import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { buildLogSVG, buildRangeSVG, taxaOptions, windowCSV } from '../../app/js/log.js';

const classes = [{ name: 'Chondrites', index: 0, color: '#123456' }, { name: 'Planolites', index: 1, color: '#654321' }];
const rows = [{ a0: 0, a1: 1, pct: 50, classPct: [20, 30] }, { a0: 1, a1: 2, pct: 40, classPct: [10, 30] }];
const base = { from: 0, to: 2, height: 200, rows, classes, records: [], tracks: { species: true },
  colWidths: { species: 100, 'sp:Chondrites': 100, 'sp:Planolites': 100 } };
const abundanceGroups = (svg) => [...svg.matchAll(/<g data-taxon="[^"]*" data-role="abundance"[^>]*>(.*?)<\/g>/gs)].map((match) => match[1]);

test('figure composition hides and reorders taxon tracks alongside proxies without losing available columns', () => {
  const input = { ...base, taxa: { layout: 'separate' }, respectTaxonColumnVisibility: true,
    records: [{ name: 'Ca', pts: [[0, 2], [2, 4]] }],
    style: { colHide: ['sp:Planolites'], colOrder: ['rec:Ca', 'sp:Chondrites', 'sp:Planolites'] } };
  const result = buildLogSVG(input);
  assert.deepEqual(result.layout.map((column) => column.id), ['rec:Ca', 'sp:Chondrites']);
  assert.ok(result.columns.some((column) => column.id === 'sp:Planolites'));
  assert.equal(result.svg.includes('data-taxon="Planolites"'), false);
  assert.equal(result.svg.includes('>Planolites</text>'), false);
  assert.equal(buildLogSVG({ ...input, taxa: { ...input.taxa, hidden: [] }, respectTaxonColumnVisibility: false }).layout.some((column) => column.id === 'sp:Planolites'), true);
});

test('stacked and overlaid abundances retain exact percentage widths and change only the baseline', () => {
  for (const layout of ['stack', 'overlay']) {
    const result = buildLogSVG({ ...base, taxa: { layout, scale: 'percent', opacity: 0.4 } });
    const column = result.layout.find((item) => item.key === 'species');
    const groups = abundanceGroups(result.svg);
    const rectangles = groups.map((group) => [...group.matchAll(/<rect x="([^"]+)"[^>]*width="([^"]+)"/g)].map((match) => [+match[1], +match[2]]));
    assert.deepEqual(rectangles[0], [[column.x, 20], [column.x, 10]]);
    assert.deepEqual(rectangles[1], [[column.x + (layout === 'stack' ? 20 : 0), 30], [column.x + (layout === 'stack' ? 10 : 0), 30]]);
    assert.match(result.svg, /data-role="abundance" opacity="0.4"/);
    assert.match(result.svg, />100%<\/text>/);
  }
});

test('symmetric abundance curves use half the magnitude on each side and split at missing intervals and gaps', () => {
  const input = [...rows, { a0: 3, a1: 4, pct: 20, classPct: [20, 0] },
    { a0: 4, a1: 5, pct: null, classPct: [null, null] }, { a0: 5, a1: 6, pct: 40, classPct: [40, 0] }];
  const result = buildLogSVG({ ...base, rows: input, to: 6, taxa: { layout: 'separate', abundance: 'kite', scale: 'percent' } });
  const center = result.layout.find((column) => column.id === 'sp:Chondrites').x + 50;
  const polygons = [...abundanceGroups(result.svg)[0].matchAll(/<polygon points="([^"]*)"/g)].map((match) => match[1].split(' ').map((pair) => pair.split(',').map(Number)));
  assert.equal(polygons.length, 3);
  assert.equal(Math.max(...polygons[2].map(([x]) => x)) - center, 20);
  for (const polygon of polygons) for (const [x, y] of polygon) {
    assert.ok(polygon.some(([otherX, otherY]) => Math.abs(otherX + x - 2 * center) <= 0.01 && otherY === y));
  }
  assert.doesNotMatch(result.svg, /NaN|Infinity/);
});

test('manual names retain their exact depth and never fabricate percentage data', () => {
  const result = buildLogSVG({ ...base, rows: [], taxonPoints: [{ a: 0.25, index: 0, kind: 'mark' }, { a: 0.75, index: 1, kind: 'obj' }],
    taxa: { abundance: 'none', occurrence: 'names', source: 'marks', layout: 'separate' } });
  assert.equal(result.layout.filter((column) => column.key === 'sp1').length, 1);
  const occurrence = /<g data-taxon="Chondrites" data-role="occurrence"[^>]*>(.*?)<\/g>/s.exec(result.svg)[1];
  assert.match(occurrence, /y="112"/); // top 84 + 0.25 / 2 * 200 + text baseline 3.
  assert.match(occurrence, />Chondrites<\/text>/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(occurrence, /<path/);
  assert.ok(abundanceGroups(result.svg).every((group) => group === ''));
  assert.doesNotMatch(result.svg, />100%<\/text>|Planolites/);
});

test('distribution supports the same shapes, opacity, percentages and manual-only occurrence filtering', () => {
  const points = [{ index: 0, a: 0.4, kind: 'obj' }, { index: 0, a: 0.6, kind: 'mark' }];
  for (const abundance of ['bars', 'line', 'area', 'kite']) {
    const svg = buildRangeSVG({ ...base, points, colW: 100, taxa: { layout: 'separate', abundance, opacity: 1, occurrence: 'both', source: 'marks', values: true, scale: 'percent' } }).svg;
    assert.equal((svg.match(/data-role="occurrence"/g) || []).length, 1);
    assert.match(svg, /data-kind="mark"/);
    assert.match(svg, /data-role="percentage"[^>]*>20%<\/text>/);
    assert.match(svg, /opacity="1"/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(svg, /NaN|Infinity/);
  }
  const overlay = buildRangeSVG({ ...base, points, taxa: { layout: 'overlay', abundance: 'area', occurrence: 'symbols' } });
  assert.equal(overlay.layout.filter((column) => column.key === 'species').length, 1);
});

test('rendering leaves rows, classes and exported measurements exactly unchanged', () => {
  const input = structuredClone(base);
  const snapshot = structuredClone(input);
  const csvOptions = { rows: input.rows, classes: input.classes, records: [], from: 0, to: 2, units: 'cm', ageUnits: 'ka' };
  const csv = windowCSV(csvOptions);
  for (const abundance of ['none', 'bars', 'line', 'area', 'kite']) {
    for (const layout of ['stack', 'overlay', 'separate']) {
      const taxa = { abundance, layout, occurrence: 'symbols', opacity: 0.5, values: true };
      buildLogSVG({ ...input, taxa }); buildRangeSVG({ ...input, taxa });
      assert.deepEqual(input, snapshot);
      assert.equal(windowCSV(csvOptions), csv);
    }
  }
});

test('legacy defaults remain available and new options validate untrusted display settings', () => {
  assert.equal(buildLogSVG(base).svg, buildLogSVG({ ...base, taxa: null }).svg);
  const q = taxaOptions({ layout: 'invalid', opacity: Infinity, hidden: [null, 'Chondrites'], abundance: 'kite' });
  assert.equal(q.layout, 'separate'); assert.equal(q.opacity, 1); assert.deepEqual(q.hidden, ['Chondrites']);
  const svg = buildRangeSVG({ ...base, classes: [{ name: '<taxon>', index: 0, color: '\"/><script>' }], taxa: { abundance: 'bars' } }).svg;
  assert.match(svg, /&lt;taxon&gt;/); assert.doesNotMatch(svg, /<script>/);
  assert.throws(() => buildLogSVG({ ...base, taxa: { abundance: 'bars' }, style: { axes: { species: { min: 20, max: 10 } } } }), /Invalid horizontal axis/);
});

test('curve and area geometry remain visible for a single measured interval and never join a gap', () => {
  for (const abundance of ['line', 'area']) {
    const svg = buildLogSVG({ ...base, rows: [{ a0: 0, a1: 1, pct: 20, classPct: [20, 0] }, { a0: 2, a1: 3, pct: 30, classPct: [30, 0] }],
      to: 3, taxa: { abundance, layout: 'separate' } }).svg;
    assert.equal((abundanceGroups(svg)[0].match(abundance === 'line' ? /<polyline/g : /<polygon/g) || []).length, 2);
  }
});

test('percentage labels can accompany presence without a filled abundance shape and remain opaque', () => {
  const result = buildLogSVG({ ...base, taxonPoints: [{ a: 0.25, index: 0, kind: 'mark' }],

  assert.match(result.svg, /data-role="percentage"[^>]*>20%<\/text>/);
  assert.equal((result.svg.match(/data-role="occurrence"/g) || []).length, 1);
});

test('fixed percentage scales override inherited bounds while names at the window endpoints stay inside', () => {
  const result = buildLogSVG({ ...base, taxonPoints: [{ a: 0, index: 0, kind: 'mark' }, { a: 2, index: 0, kind: 'mark' }],
    taxa: { layout: 'separate', abundance: 'kite', occurrence: 'names', scale: 'percent' },
    style: { axes: { 'sp:Chondrites': { min: 5, max: 25, log: true } } } });
  assert.match(result.svg, />100%<\/text>/);
  const names = [...result.svg.matchAll(/data-role="occurrence"[^>]*>.*?<text[^>]*y="([^"]+)"/gs)].map((match) => +match[1]);
  assert.ok(names.every((y) => y > result.plotTop && y < result.plotTop + result.plotHeight));
});


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/app/tiff.test.mjs
##########################################

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { deflateSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { decodeTiffBlocks } from '../../app/js/tiff.js';
import { applyFilters, autoLevels, DEFAULT_FILTERS } from '../../app/js/imageproc.js';

function tiffFixture({ big = false, little = true, tiled = false, rgb = false, virtual = false, signed = false } = {}) {
  const fw = 5, fh = 4, channels = rgb ? 3 : 1, bw = tiled ? 3 : fw, bh = 2;
  const across = Math.ceil(fw / bw), blocks = [], compression = tiled ? 8 : 1;
  const sample = (x, y, c) => 32768 + x * 7 + y * 25 + c * 120;
  for (let by = 0; by < fh / bh; by++) for (let bx = 0; bx < across; bx++) {
    const data = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(bw * bh * channels * 2));
    for (let y = 0; y < bh; y++) for (let x = 0; x < bw; x++) for (let c = 0; c < channels; c++) {
      const value = tiled && x > 0 ? 7 : sample(bx * bw + x, by * bh + y, c);
      data.setUint16(((y * bw + x) * channels + c) * 2, value, little);
    }
    blocks.push(tiled ? deflateSync(new Uint8Array(data.buffer)) : new Uint8Array(data.buffer));
  }
  const offsetTag = tiled ? 324 : 273, lengthTag = tiled ? 325 : 279;
  const long = big ? 16 : 4;
  const fields = [
    [256, 4, [fw]], [257, 4, [fh]], [258, 3, new Array(channels).fill(16)], [259, 3, [compression]],
    [262, 3, [rgb ? 2 : 1]], [277, 3, [channels]], [317, 3, [tiled ? 2 : 1]], [339, 3, [signed ? 2 : 1]],
    [offsetTag, long, blocks.map(() => 0)], [lengthTag, long, blocks.map((b) => b.length)],
    ...(tiled ? [[322, 4, [bw]], [323, 4, [bh]]] : [[278, 4, [bh]]]),
  ].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
  const word = big ? 8 : 4, ifd = big ? 16 : 8, count = big ? 8 : 2, entry = big ? 20 : 12;
  const metadata = new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(2048));
  function put(o, n, value) {
    if (n === 2) metadata.setUint16(o, value, little);
    else if (n === 4) metadata.setUint32(o, value, little);
    else metadata.setBigUint64(o, BigInt(value), little);
  }
  metadata.setUint16(0, little ? 0x4949 : 0x4d4d);
  put(2, 2, big ? 43 : 42);
  if (big) put(4, 2, 8);
  put(big ? 8 : 4, word, ifd);
  put(ifd, count, fields.length);
  let dataOffset = virtual ? 2 ** 32 + 2048 : metadata.byteLength;
  const ranges = [[0, new Uint8Array(metadata.buffer)]];
  for (const block of blocks) { ranges.push([dataOffset, block]); dataOffset += block.length; }
  let extra = ifd + count + fields.length * entry + word;
  fields.forEach(([tag, type, values], i) => {
    const pos = ifd + count + i * entry, valuePos = pos + 4 + word;
    const width = { 3: 2, 4: 4, 16: 8 }[type];
    const raw = tag === offsetTag ? ranges.slice(1).map(([offset]) => offset) : values;
    put(pos, 2, tag); put(pos + 2, 2, type); put(pos + 4, word, raw.length);
    if (raw.length * width <= word) raw.forEach((v, j) => put(valuePos + j * width, width, v));
    else {
      extra = Math.ceil(extra / word) * word;
      put(valuePos, word, extra);
      raw.forEach((v, j) => put(extra + j * width, width, v));
      extra += raw.length * width;
    }
  });
  const reads = [];
  const source = {
    size: dataOffset,
    slice(start, end) {
      reads.push([start, end]);
      const range = ranges.find(([offset, bytes]) => start >= offset && end <= offset + bytes.length);
      assert.ok(range, `unexpected range ${start}..${end}`);
      const [offset, bytes] = range;
      return new Blob([bytes.subarray(start - offset, end - offset)]);
    },
  };
  return { source, sample, reads };
}

const wholeTiff = (w, h) => ({ x: 0, y: 0, w, h });

test('16-bit TIFF strips preserve neighboring samples and drive automatic levels', async () => {
  for (const little of [false, true]) {
    const { source, sample } = tiffFixture({ little });
    const { rgba, rgba16, w, h } = await decodeTiffBlocks(source, wholeTiff, () => 1);
    assert.deepEqual([w, h], [5, 4]);
    assert.equal(rgba[0], rgba[4]);
    assert.equal(rgba16[0], sample(0, 0, 0));
    assert.equal(rgba16[4], sample(1, 0, 0));
    const levels = autoLevels(rgba16, w, h);
    const out = applyFilters(rgba16, w, h, { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, ...levels });
    assert.ok(out[4] > out[0]);
  }
});

test('signed grayscale TIFF keeps the 16-bit samples in an offset working domain', async () => {
  const { source } = tiffFixture({ signed: true });
  const { rgba16 } = await decodeTiffBlocks(source, wholeTiff, () => 1);
  assert.deepEqual([...rgba16.subarray(0, 8)], [0, 0, 0, 65535, 7, 7, 7, 65535]);
});

test('BigTIFF ranges beyond 4 GiB decode Deflate tiles and average across their boundary', async () => {
  const { source, sample, reads } = tiffFixture({ big: true, little: false, tiled: true, rgb: true, virtual: true });
  const { rgba16, w, h } = await decodeTiffBlocks(source, () => ({ x: 2, y: 1, w: 2, h: 2 }), () => 2);
  assert.deepEqual([w, h], [1, 1]);
  const expected = [0, 1, 2].map((c) => Math.round(
    (sample(2, 1, c) + sample(3, 1, c) + sample(2, 2, c) + sample(3, 2, c)) / 4
  ));
  assert.deepEqual([...rgba16], [...expected, 65535]);
  assert.equal(reads.filter(([offset]) => offset > 2 ** 32).length, 4);
  assert.ok(reads.every(([start, end]) => end - start <= 2048));
});

test('a crop only reads intersecting strips and rejects truncated ranges', async () => {
  const { source, reads } = tiffFixture();
  const result = await decodeTiffBlocks(source, () => ({ x: 0, y: 2, w: 1, h: 1 }), () => 1);
  assert.equal(result.rgba16[0], 32818);
  assert.ok(reads.every(([offset]) => offset !== 2048));
  await assert.rejects(decodeTiffBlocks({ ...source, size: source.size - 1 }, wholeTiff, () => 1), /TIFF block range/);
});

import test from 'node:test';

test('the managed trainer resolves current bundled Python code before its installed copy', () => {
  const root = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'sedcoretool-trainer-'));
  const pySrc = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
  try {
    const managed = new Trainer({ root, pySrc, python: null });
    assert.equal(managed.pythonEnv().PYTHONPATH.split(path.delimiter)[0], path.join(pySrc, 'src'));
    const external = new Trainer({ root, pySrc, python: 'external-python' });
    assert.equal(external.pythonEnv().PYTHONPATH, process.env.PYTHONPATH);

    rmdirSync(path.join(root, 'models'));
    rmdirSync(path.join(root, 'runs'));
    rmdirSync(root);

ARCHIVO: tests/app/unet.test.mjs
##########################################

// V0.15: U-Net tiling, blending, resampling and training masks; parity with src/sedcoretool/unet/tiling.py
// through tests/fixtures/unet_parity.json (written by tests/fixtures/make_unet_parity.py).
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tileStarts, blendWindow, resampleBilinear, predictProb, trainingMask, objectClasses, IGNORE } from '../../app/js/unet.js';
import { detectMask, DEFAULT_DETECT } from '../../app/js/imageproc.js';

const REF = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('../fixtures/unet_parity.json', import.meta.url)));
const { w: W, h: H } = REF, [rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1] = REF.roi, ROI = { x0: rx0, y0: ry0, x1: rx1, y1: ry1 };
function image() {
  const a = new Uint8ClampedArray(W * H * 4);
  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) { const j = (y * W + x) * 4; a[j] = (x * 37 + y * 11) % 256; a[j + 1] = (x * 5 + y * 29) % 256; a[j + 2] = (x * y) % 256; a[j + 3] = 255; }
  return a;
}
const runner = (C) => async (inp, T) => {
  const n = T * T, out = new Float32Array(C * n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (C === 1) out[i] = inp[i] - 0.5 * inp[2 * n + i]; else for (let c = 0; c < C; c++) out[c * n + i] = inp[c * n + i]; }
  return { data: out, channels: C };
};
const roiValues = (arr) => { const o = []; for (let y = ROI.y0; y < ROI.y1; y++) for (let x = ROI.x0; x < ROI.x1; x++) o.push(arr[y * W + x]); return o; };

test('tile starts and blending window match Python', () => {
  assert.deepEqual([[10, 16, 4], [16, 16, 4], [17, 16, 4], [100, 32, 8], [95, 32, 8]].map(([n, t, o]) => tileStarts(n, t, o)), REF.tileStarts);
  [...blendWindow(8, 3)].forEach((v, i) => assert.ok(Math.abs(v - REF.blend[i]) < 1e-6));
});

test('bilinear resampling matches Python exactly (uint8)', () => {
  const rgb = new Uint8ClampedArray(W * H * 3), a = image();
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) rgb[i * 3 + c] = a[i * 4 + c];
  assert.deepEqual([...resampleBilinear(rgb, W, H, 3, 31, 23)], REF.resampled);
});

test('probability maps match Python (binary with rescaling; multi-class)', async () => {
  const p1 = await predictProb(runner(1), image(), W, H, ROI, { tile: REF.tile, overlap: REF.overlap }, 1.3);
  roiValues(p1.prob).forEach((v, i) => assert.ok(Math.abs(v - REF.prob1[i]) < 2e-4, `prob1[${i}] ${v} vs ${REF.prob1[i]}`));
  const p3 = await predictProb(runner(3), image(), W, H, ROI, { tile: REF.tile, overlap: REF.overlap }, 1);
  roiValues(p3.prob).forEach((v, i) => assert.ok(Math.abs(v - REF.prob3[i]) < 2e-4));
  assert.deepEqual(roiValues(p3.cls), REF.cls3);
  assert.equal(p1.prob[0], 0);   // outside the ROI
});

test('training mask: classes, matrix, ignored excluded pixels and unreviewed objects', () => {
  const w = 4, labels = new Int32Array([0, 1, 1, 2, 0, 3, 0, 0]), exclude = new Uint8Array([0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0]);
  const objLabel = new Int16Array([-1, 2, -1, 0]);   // object 1 class 2, object 2 unreviewed, object 3 artefact
  assert.deepEqual([...trainingMask(labels, w, { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: 4, y1: 2 }, exclude, objLabel)], [0, 2, 2, IGNORE, IGNORE, 0, 0, 0]);
  assert.deepEqual([...trainingMask(labels, w, { x0: 1, y0: 0, x1: 3, y1: 2 }, null, objLabel)], [2, 2, 0, 0]);
});

test('unet detection thresholds the probability map inside the ROI and outside excluded zones', () => {
  const w = 3, h = 2, prob = new Float32Array([0.9, 0.4, 0.6, 0.9, 0.9, 0.2]), ex = new Uint8Array([0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0]);
  const p = { ...DEFAULT_DETECT, method: 'unet', smooth: 0, pThr: 0.5 };
  assert.deepEqual([...detectMask(null, w, h, { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: 3, y1: 2 }, ex, p, prob)], [1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0]);
  assert.deepEqual([...detectMask(null, w, h, { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: 2, y1: 2 }, ex, p, prob)], [1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0]);
  assert.deepEqual([...detectMask(null, w, h, { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: 3, y1: 2 }, ex, p, null)], [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
});

test('objectClasses gives the majority U-Net class per object', () => {
  const r = objectClasses(new Int32Array([1, 1, 1, 2, 0]), new Uint8Array([2, 2, 1, 1, 2]), new Set([1, 2]));
  assert.deepEqual(r.get(1), { k: 2, frac: 2 / 3 }); assert.deepEqual(r.get(2), { k: 1, frac: 1 });
});

// V0.49: automatic comparison of the web analysis with the reference output of V2.1 (legacy/v2.1_console),
// as the Python package already does (tests/test_core.py). Mask decoded once with Pillow (tools/make_v2_fixture.py).
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { rleDecode, rowCounts, binRows } from '../../app/js/core.js';
import { parseTable } from '../../app/js/log.js';

const R = (p) => readFileSync(new URL('../../' + p, import.meta.url), 'utf8');
test('V0.49: web binning and indices reproduce the V2.1 reference CSV exactly', () => {
  const fx = JSON.parse(R('tests/app/fixtures/v2_idealized_masks.json'));
  const labels = rleDecode(fx.mask, fx.w * fx.h);
  const counts = rowCounts({ labels, width: fx.w, roi: { x0: 0, x1: fx.w, y0: 0, y1: fx.h }, exclude: null, effClass: [-1, 0], nClasses: 1 });
  const bins = binRows(counts, { top: fx.top, bottom: fx.bottom, binPx: fx.binPx });
  const v2 = parseTable(R('legacy/v2.1_console/example_output.csv'));
  const col = (n) => v2.headers.indexOf(n);
  assert.equal(v2.rows.length, 110); assert.ok(bins.length >= 110);   // V2 dropped the last, partial interval
  v2.rows.forEach((r, i) => {
    assert.ok(Math.abs(bins[i].depth - r[col('Depth')]) < 1e-9, `depth ${i}`);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(bins[i].pct - r[col('Bioturbation Percentage')]) < 1e-9, `pct ${i}: ${bins[i].pct} vs ${r[col('Bioturbation Percentage')]}`);
    assert.equal(bins[i].BI, r[col('BI')], `BI ${i}`); assert.equal(bins[i].ii, r[col('II')], `ii ${i}`);
  });
});

ARCHIVO: tests/app/vision.test.mjs
##########################################

// V0.17: vision-model descriptors for ichnotaxa (window, input, embedding) and classifier with any vector length.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { VISION_MODEL, visionFeatureId, traceWindow, traceInput, pickEmbedding } from '../../app/js/vision.js';
import { Classifier, TaxonModel, FEATURES } from '../../app/js/learn.js';

test('square window around the trace with context, centred', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(traceWindow([10, 20, 19, 59], 0.25), { x: -15, y: 10, side: 60 });   // 10 × 40 box → side 40·1.5
  assert.equal(traceWindow([5, 5, 5, 5], 0.25, 16).side, 16);                          // minimum size
});

test('network input: size, channel order and ImageNet normalisation', () => {
  const w = 4, h = 4, rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(w * h * 4);
  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) { rgba[i * 4] = 255; rgba[i * 4 + 1] = 0; rgba[i * 4 + 2] = 128; rgba[i * 4 + 3] = 255; }
  const m = { ...VISION_MODEL, size: 8 }, x = traceInput(rgba, w, h, { x: -2, y: -2, side: 8 }, m), n = 64;
  assert.equal(x.length, 3 * n);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(x[0] - (1 - 0.485) / 0.229) < 1e-5);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(x[n] - (0 - 0.456) / 0.224) < 1e-5);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(x[2 * n + 63] - (128 / 255 - 0.406) / 0.225) < 1e-5);   // edge padding outside the image
});

test('embedding: pooler output preferred, else class token; unit length', () => {
  const tokens = { data: new Float32Array([3, 4, 9, 9, 9, 9]), dims: [1, 3, 2] };
  assert.deepEqual(pickEmbedding({ last_hidden_state: tokens }, ['last_hidden_state']), [0.6, 0.8]);
  assert.deepEqual(pickEmbedding({ last_hidden_state: tokens, pooler_output: { data: new Float32Array([0, 2]), dims: [1, 2] } }, ['last_hidden_state', 'pooler_output']), [0, 1]);
});

test('classifier learns from vision vectors and keeps feature sets apart', () => {
  const D = 384, ex = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { const x = new Array(D).fill(0); x[i % 2 ? 0 : 1] = 1; x[5 + (i % 7)] = 0.1; ex.push({ y: i % 2 ? 'A' : 'B', x }); }
  const m = new TaxonModel({ features: visionFeatureId() }).fit(ex);
  const q = new Array(D).fill(0); q[0] = 1; q[6] = 0.1;
  assert.equal(m.predict(q).y, 'A');
  const j = m.toJSON(); assert.deepEqual(j.features, visionFeatureId());
  assert.equal(Classifier.fromJSON(j).n, 0);                          // shape model expected: not loaded
  assert.equal(Classifier.fromJSON(j, visionFeatureId()).n, 20);
  assert.equal(new Classifier().features, FEATURES);
});


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/app/zip.test.mjs
##########################################

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { deflateRawSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { zipFiles, unzipBlob, crc32 } from '../../app/js/zip.js';

test('V0.8: zip round trip (text and binary entries) and CRC-32', async () => {
  assert.equal(crc32(new TextEncoder().encode('123456789')), 0xcbf43926);
  const bin = new Uint8Array(3000).map((_, i) => (i * 7) & 255);
  const z = await zipFiles([{ name: 'project.json', data: '{"a":1}' }, { name: 'images/x ñ.jpg', data: new Blob([bin]) }]);
  const m = await unzipBlob(z);
  assert.equal(await (await m.get('project.json').get()).text(), '{"a":1}');
  assert.deepEqual(new Uint8Array(await (await m.get('images/x ñ.jpg').get()).arrayBuffer()), bin);
});

test('ZIP computes the CRC across bounded slices without reading the original as one buffer', async () => {
  class SliceOnlyBlob extends Blob {
    arrayBuffer() { throw new Error('whole input read'); }
  }
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(4 * 1024 * 1024 + 23);
  bytes.set([12, 34, 56, 78], 4 * 1024 * 1024 - 2);
  const zip = await zipFiles([{ name: 'images/core.tif', data: new SliceOnlyBlob([bytes]) }]);
  const header = new DataView(await zip.slice(0, 30).arrayBuffer());
  assert.equal(header.getUint32(14, true), crc32(bytes));
  const result = await (await unzipBlob(zip)).get('images/core.tif').get();
  assert.equal(result.size, bytes.length);
  assert.deepEqual(new Uint8Array(await result.slice(-25, -21).arrayBuffer()), new Uint8Array([12, 34, 56, 78]));
});

test('ZIP checks stored payloads in bounded reads and returns a sliceable Blob', async () => {
  const blockSize = 4 * 1024 ** 2, reads = [];
  class BoundedBlob extends Blob {
    slice(...args) { return new BoundedBlob([super.slice(...args)]); }
    arrayBuffer() {
      assert.ok(this.size <= blockSize, 'read exceeds the CRC block size');
      reads.push(this.size);
      return super.arrayBuffer();
    }
  }
  const payload = new Uint8Array(blockSize + 23);
  payload.set([12, 34, 56, 78], blockSize - 2);
  const name = 'images/core.tif';
  const zip = await zipFiles([{ name, data: payload }]);
  const entries = await unzipBlob(new BoundedBlob([zip]));
  reads.length = 0;
  const member = await entries.get(name).get();
  assert.deepEqual(reads.slice(-2), [blockSize, 23]);
  assert.equal(member.size, payload.length);
  assert.deepEqual(new Uint8Array(await member.slice(blockSize - 2, blockSize + 2).arrayBuffer()), new Uint8Array([12, 34, 56, 78]));
});

for (const [name, data, offset] of [
  ['project.json', '{"name":"Core"}', 9],
  ['images/core.png', new Uint8Array([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47]), 1],
]) {
  test(`ZIP rejects a stored ${name} with one altered payload byte`, async () => {
    const zip = await zipFiles([{ name, data }]);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(await zip.arrayBuffer()), header = new DataView(bytes.buffer);
    const start = 30 + header.getUint16(26, true) + header.getUint16(28, true);
    bytes[start + offset] ^= 1;
    const entries = await unzipBlob(new Blob([bytes]));
    await assert.rejects(entries.get(name).get(), /CRC mismatch/);
  });
}

async function deflatedMember(data, size = data.length) {
  const blob = await zipFiles([{ name: 'project.json', data: deflateRawSync(data) }]);
  const view = new DataView(await blob.arrayBuffer()), cd = view.getUint32(view.byteLength - 6, true);
  view.setUint16(8, 8, true); view.setUint16(cd + 10, 8, true);
  view.setUint32(14, crc32(data), true); view.setUint32(cd + 16, crc32(data), true);
  view.setUint32(22, size, true); view.setUint32(cd + 24, size, true);
  return new Blob([view.buffer]);
}

test('ZIP accepts valid deflate and rejects a forged low or oversized expanded size', async () => {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({ a: 'A'.repeat(64 * 1024) }));
  const valid = await unzipBlob(await deflatedMember(data));
  assert.deepEqual(new Uint8Array(await (await valid.get('project.json').get()).arrayBuffer()), data);
  const forged = await unzipBlob(await deflatedMember(data, 64));
  await assert.rejects(forged.get('project.json').get(), /inflated size/);
  await assert.rejects(unzipBlob(await deflatedMember(data, 129 * 1024**2)), /uncompressed size/);
});

test('ZIP rejects unsafe paths, duplicate names and overlapping member offsets', async () => {
  await assert.rejects(unzipBlob(await zipFiles([{ name: '../project.json', data: '{}' }])), /member name/);
  const repeated = [{ name: 'project.json', data: '{}' }, { name: 'project.json', data: '{}' }];
  await assert.rejects(unzipBlob(await zipFiles(repeated)), /duplicate/);
  const blob = await zipFiles([{ name: 'a', data: 'A' }, { name: 'b', data: 'B' }]);
  const view = new DataView(await blob.arrayBuffer()), cd = view.getUint32(view.byteLength - 6, true);
  view.setUint32(cd + 47 + 42, 0, true);
  await assert.rejects(unzipBlob(new Blob([view.buffer])), /Overlapping ZIP/);
});

import { prepareDataTableFixture, captureDataTableCSV } from '../../tools/data_table_fixture.mjs';

export async function verifyDataTable(browser, url) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
  try {
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(prepareDataTableFixture, 2400);
    const result = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const { t } = await import('./js/i18n.js');
      document.getElementById('modeData').click();
      const { S } = window.ichantool;
      const initial = document.querySelectorAll('#dBody tbody tr').length;
      const total = document.getElementById('dInfo').textContent === t('data.info', { r: 2400, c: 6 });
      let reads = 0;
      const originals = S.cores.map((core) => core.proxies);
      try {
        for (const core of S.cores) core.proxies = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(core.proxies).map(([name, values]) => [name,
          new Proxy(values, { get(target, property, receiver) { if (/^\d+$/.test(String(property))) reads++; return Reflect.get(target, property, receiver); } }),
        ]));
        document.getElementById('dsEdit').click();
      } finally { S.cores.forEach((core, index) => { core.proxies = originals[index]; }); }
      const bounded = reads === 400 * 6;
      document.getElementById('dMore').click();
      const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('#dBody tbody tr')];
      const coreBoundary = rows[1200].querySelector('[data-col="value_1"]').dataset.core === 'table-core-1'
        && rows[1200].children[0].textContent === '1201';
      const cell = rows[1].querySelector('[data-col="value_0"]');
      cell.focus();
      const range = document.createRange(); range.selectNodeContents(cell);
      const selection = window.getSelection(); selection.removeAllRanges(); selection.addRange(range);
      const selected = selection.toString() === cell.textContent && document.activeElement === cell;
      cell.textContent = '-901.234567'; cell.blur();
      const edited = S.cores[0].edits.value_0['1'].v === -901.234567;
      return { initial, expanded: rows.length, total, bounded, coreBoundary, selected, edited };
    });
    if (result.initial !== 400 || result.expanded !== 1400 || !['total', 'bounded', 'coreBoundary', 'selected', 'edited'].every((key) => result[key])) {
      throw new Error('Data table regression: ' + JSON.stringify(result));
    }
    await page.evaluate(captureDataTableCSV, { rowCount: 2400, edited: { row: 1, column: 0, value: -901.234567 } });
    await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('dsRestoreAll').click(); document.getElementById('dsRestoreAll').click(); });
    await page.evaluate(captureDataTableCSV, { rowCount: 2400 });
    const filtered = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const { t } = await import('./js/i18n.js');
      const core = document.getElementById('dCore'); core.value = 'table-core-1';
      core.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
      const correctCore = document.querySelector('#dBody td[data-col]').dataset.core === 'table-core-1'
        && document.getElementById('dInfo').textContent === t('data.info', { r: 1200, c: 6 });
      const layers = document.getElementById('dLayers'); layers.click();
      const hiddenLayers = document.querySelectorAll('#dBody th.src-lay').length === 0;
      layers.click();
      return correctCore && hiddenLayers && document.querySelectorAll('#dBody th.src-lay').length === 2
        && document.querySelectorAll('#dBody tbody tr').length === 400;
    });
    if (!filtered) throw new Error('Core/layer selection did not reset the displayed prefix correctly');
    const ageOnly = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const I = window.ichantool, { t } = await import('./js/i18n.js');
      I.setMode('section');
      await I.loadProject({ app: 'SedCoreTool', kind: 'project', version: 5, id: 'data-table-axes',
        cores: [
          { id: 'depth', name: 'Depth', imp: { depth: Array.from({ length: 250 }, (_, i) => i), proxies: { upper: Array(250).fill(7) } } },
          { id: 'empty', name: 'Empty' },
          { id: 'age', name: 'Age only', imp: { age: Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => 2 * i), proxies: { lower: Array(300).fill(9) } } },
        ] });
      const selector = document.getElementById('dCore');
      selector.innerHTML = '<option value="__all">All cores</option>'; selector.value = '__all';
      I.setMode('data');
      const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#dBody tbody tr');
      return rows.length === 400 && rows[250].querySelector('[data-col="lower"]').dataset.core === 'age'
        && rows[250].querySelector('[data-col="lower"]').dataset.x === '0'
        && rows[0].querySelector('[data-col="lower"]').textContent === ''
        && document.getElementById('dInfo').textContent === t('data.info', { r: 550, c: 2 });
    });
    if (!ageOnly) throw new Error('Empty or age-only cores changed table boundaries, columns or row counts');
    const intervalLabels = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const I = window.ichantool, { parseTable } = await import('./js/log.js');
      I.setMode('section');
      const values = Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => i);
      const layers = ['disjoint', 'overlap'].map((id) => ({ id, name: id,
        cats: ['Early', 'Middle', 'Late', 'First', 'Second'].map((name) => ({ id: id + name, name, color: '#567890' })) }));
      const intervals = ['depth', 'age'].flatMap((core) => {
        const scale = core === 'depth' ? 2 : 1;
        return [['disjoint', 6, 8, 'Late'], ['disjoint', 0, 2, 'Early'], ['disjoint', 2, 4, 'Middle'],
          ['overlap', 2, 8, 'First'], ['overlap', 0, 6, 'Second']].map(([layer, from, to, category], index) => ({
          id: core + index, core, layer, from: from * scale, to: to * scale, cat: layer + category, axis: 'age',
        }));
      });
      await I.loadProject({ app: 'SedCoreTool', kind: 'project', version: 5, id: 'data-table-intervals',
        cores: [{ id: 'depth', name: 'Depth', imp: { depth: values, age: values.map((x) => x * 2), proxies: { A: values } } },
          { id: 'age', name: 'Age', imp: { age: values, proxies: { A: values } } }], geo: { layers, intervals } });
      const selector = document.getElementById('dCore');
      selector.innerHTML = '<option value="__all">All cores</option>'; selector.value = '__all';
      document.getElementById('dLayers').checked = true;
      I.setMode('data');
      const disjoint = ['Early', 'Early', 'Middle', 'Middle', '', '', 'Late', 'Late', '', '', ''];
      const overlap = ['Second', 'Second', 'First', 'First', 'First', 'First', 'First', 'First', '', '', ''];
      const expected = [...values, ...values].map((x) => [disjoint[x], overlap[x]]);
      const displayed = [...document.querySelectorAll('#dBody tbody tr')].map((row) => [...row.children].slice(-2).map((cell) => cell.textContent));
      const createURL = URL.createObjectURL;
      const preventDownload = (event) => { if (event.target.closest?.('a[download]')) event.preventDefault(); };
      let csv;
      document.addEventListener('click', preventDownload, true);
      URL.createObjectURL = (blob) => { if (blob.type === 'text/csv') csv = blob; return createURL(blob); };
      try {
        document.getElementById('dCsv').click();
        const exported = parseTable(await csv.text()).text.map((row) => row.slice(-2));
        return JSON.stringify(displayed) === JSON.stringify(expected) && JSON.stringify(exported) === JSON.stringify(expected);
      } finally { URL.createObjectURL = createURL; document.removeEventListener('click', preventDownload, true); }
    });
    if (!intervalLabels) throw new Error('Table/CSV interval labels changed at overlaps, boundaries, gaps or axis conversion');
    if (errors.length) throw new Error('Data table page errors: ' + errors.join(' | '));
    console.log('Data table: bounded preparation, selection, edit/restore, complete CSV and interval labels verified.');
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}

// Real desktop entry point with an isolated test profile and hidden windows.
const { app } = require('electron');

if (!profile) throw new Error('Missing isolated test profile');

app.on('window-all-closed', () => app.quit());

##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/desktop.mjs
##########################################

// V0.48: actual Electron, app://, worker, preload IPC and save-on-close across process restarts.
// Needs desktop/npm ci + prepare-web and puppeteer-core at the repository root.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
const { default: puppeteer } = await import(process.env.PUPPETEER_MODULE || 'puppeteer-core');
const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
const requireDesktop = createRequire(path.join(root, 'desktop/package.json'));
const executable = requireDesktop('electron');
const profile = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'sedcoretool-desktop-test-'));
const errors = [];
const signature = () => window.ichantool.S.sections.map((s) => `${s.objects.size}:${s.labels.reduce((a, v) => a + !!v, 0)}`).join(',');

async function start() {
  const env = { ...process.env, SEDCORETOOL_TEST_PROFILE: profile };
  delete env.ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE;
  const child = spawn(executable, [path.join(root, 'tests/e2e/desktop-bootstrap.cjs'), '--headless', '--no-sandbox', '--remote-debugging-port=0'], { env, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
  let log = '';
  const exited = new Promise((resolve) => child.once('exit', resolve));
  const endpoint = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Electron startup timed out: ' + log.slice(-1500))), 60000);
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('exit', (code) => { clearTimeout(timeout); reject(new Error(`Electron exited (${code}): ${log.slice(-1500)}`)); });
    child.stderr.on('data', (bytes) => {
      log += bytes;
      const hit = log.match(/DevTools listening on (ws:\/\/[^\s]+)/);
      if (hit) { clearTimeout(timeout); resolve(hit[1]); }
    });
    child.stdout.on('data', (bytes) => { log += bytes; });
  }).catch((error) => { child.kill(); throw error; });
  const browser = await puppeteer.connect({ browserWSEndpoint: endpoint, defaultViewport: null });
  const target = await browser.waitForTarget((t) => t.url().startsWith('app://corantool/app/index.html'), { timeout: 60000 });
  const page = await target.page();
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(e.message));
  page.on('dialog', (dialog) => dialog.accept().catch((e) => {
    // Electron may finish its deferred save and close before CDP can accept beforeunload.
    if (dialog.type() !== 'beforeunload') errors.push(e.message);
  }));
  await page.waitForFunction(() => !!window.ichantool, { timeout: 60000 });
  return { child, exited, browser, page };
}

async function close(run) {
  // Close the real BrowserWindow. beforeunload must finish pending saves before the process exits.
  await run.page.close({ runBeforeUnload: true });
  let timer;
  try {
    const code = await Promise.race([run.exited, new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Electron did not finish saving and close')), 30000); })]);
    assert.equal(code, 0, 'Electron exit code');
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); run.browser.disconnect(); }
}

let run;
try {
  run = await start();
  const p = run.page;
  const desktop = await p.evaluate(async () => ({
    origin: location.origin,
    trainer: await window.corantoolDesktop.trainer.status(),
    ort: (await fetch('vendor/ort/ort.wasm.min.js')).ok,
    csp: !!document.querySelector('meta[http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy"]'),
  }));
  assert.equal(desktop.origin, 'app://corantool');
  assert.ok(!desktop.trainer.busy, 'trainer is idle');
  assert.ok(desktop.ort, 'bundled ONNX runtime');
  assert.ok(desktop.csp, 'CSP present');
  // Optional real-model/network checks use a local pinned ONNX file and a fixed public map extent.
  // CI remains independent of external services and a large model download.
  if (process.env.SEDCORETOOL_VISION_MODEL) {
    await p.addScriptTag({ url: 'app://corantool/app/vendor/ort/ort.wasm.min.js' });
    await p.evaluate(() => { const input = document.createElement('input'); input.type = 'file'; input.id = 'smokeModel'; document.body.append(input); });
    await (await p.$('#smokeModel')).uploadFile(process.env.SEDCORETOOL_VISION_MODEL);
    const vision = await p.evaluate(async () => {
      const m = await import('app://corantool/app/js/vision.js');
      const bytes = await document.getElementById('smokeModel').files[0].arrayBuffer();
      const sha = Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes)), (v) => v.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
      if (sha !== m.VISION_MODEL.sha256) throw new Error('DINOv2 SHA-256 mismatch');
      window.ort.env.wasm.wasmPaths = 'app://corantool/app/vendor/ort/'; window.ort.env.wasm.numThreads = 1;
      const session = await window.ort.InferenceSession.create(bytes, { executionProviders: ['wasm'] });
      const rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(32 * 32 * 4);
      for (let i = 0; i < rgba.length; i++) rgba[i] = i % 4 === 3 ? 255 : (i * 17) % 256;
      const input = m.traceInput(rgba, 32, 32, m.traceWindow([3, 4, 25, 27]));
      const output = await session.run({ [session.inputNames[0]]: new window.ort.Tensor('float32', input, [1, 3, 224, 224]) });
      const embedding = m.pickEmbedding(output, session.outputNames);
      await session.release();
      return { sha, dim: embedding.length, finite: embedding.every(Number.isFinite), norm: Math.hypot(...embedding) };
    });
    assert.equal(vision.dim, 384); assert.ok(vision.finite); assert.ok(Math.abs(vision.norm - 1) < 0.001);
    console.log('DINOv2 real-model smoke ok:', JSON.stringify(vision));
  }
  if (process.env.SEDCORETOOL_NETWORK_SMOKE === '1') {
    const maps = await p.evaluate(async () => {
      const services = ['World_Imagery', 'World_Topo_Map', 'World_Shaded_Relief', 'World_Physical_Map', 'World_Street_Map', 'Ocean/World_Ocean_Base'];
      return Promise.all(services.map(async (service) => {
        const url = `https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/${service}/MapServer/export?bbox=0,35,5,40&bboxSR=4326&imageSR=4326&size=256,256&format=jpg&f=image`;
        const response = await fetch(url); if (!response.ok) throw new Error(service + ': HTTP ' + response.status);
        const image = await createImageBitmap(await response.blob());
        return { service, width: image.width, height: image.height };
      }));
    });
    for (const map of maps) { assert.equal(map.width, 256); assert.equal(map.height, 256); }
    console.log('Esri network smoke ok:', JSON.stringify(maps));
  }
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('openTest').click());
  await p.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool.S.sections.length === 6 && window.ichantool.S.sections.every((s) => s.objects.size > 0), { timeout: 90000 });
  await p.waitForFunction(() => document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state === 'saved', { timeout: 30000 });
  const before = await p.evaluate(signature);
  // An edit immediately followed by close exercises the desktop's deferred beforeunload save.
  const deleted = await p.evaluate(() => {
    const I = window.ichantool, s = I.S.sections[I.S.cur], id = s.objects.keys().next().value;
    I.S.selected = id;
    document.body.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Delete', bubbles: true }));
    return !s.objects.has(id);
  });
  assert.ok(deleted, 'trace deleted before closing');
  const edited = await p.evaluate(signature);
  assert.notEqual(edited, before);
  await close(run); run = null;
  run = await start();
  await run.page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool.S.sections.length === 6, { timeout: 60000 });
  assert.equal(await run.page.evaluate(signature), edited, 'pending edit survived a full process restart');
  assert.deepEqual(errors, [], 'renderer errors');
  await close(run); run = null;
  console.log('Desktop E2E ok: app://, CSP, bundled ORT, trainer IPC, detection and pending save across restart');
} finally {
  if (run) { run.browser.disconnect(); run.child.kill(); }
  // Profiles contain only public example data and remain in the temporary directory for diagnosis.
}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { multiproxyFigureFixture } from './multiproxy-figure.mjs';

/** Independent figure settings, nonlinear image/occurrence mapping and saved-profile recovery. */
export async function verifyFigureComposition(browser, url, { screenshots = false } = {}) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.setDefaultTimeout(15000); page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
  const change = (selector, value) => page.evaluate((selector, value) => {
    const input = document.querySelector(selector);
    if (!input) throw new Error('Missing composition control: ' + selector);
    if (input.type === 'checkbox') input.checked = value; else input.value = String(value);
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
  }, selector, value);
  const click = (selector) => page.evaluate((selector) => document.querySelector(selector).click(), selector);
  const taxa = (key, value) => change(`[data-taxa-scope="figure"] [data-taxa="${key}"]`, value);
  const globals = () => page.evaluate(() => {
    const S = window.ichantool.S;
    return JSON.stringify({ log: S.logView, geo: S.geo, core: S.curCore, age: S.age, opacity: S.ovAlpha,
      data: S.cores.map((core) => ({ imp: core.imp, proxies: core.proxies, sections: core.sections.map((section) => ({
        labels: [...section.labels], exclude: [...section.exclude], marks: section.marks, bins: section.bins,
        assignments: [...section.objects.values()].map((object) => [object.id, object.cls]) })) })) });
  });
  const type = async (next) => {
    const previous = await page.$eval('#figOut', (element) => element.innerHTML);
    await change('#figType', next);
    await page.waitForFunction((previous) => document.querySelector('#figOut svg') && document.getElementById('figOut').innerHTML !== previous, {}, previous);
  };
  try {
    const fixture = multiproxyFigureFixture();
    fixture.cores[0].logView.textEdits = { '["Cu",0]': { text: 'Copper', x: 3, y: 0 } };
    fixture.cores[0].age.text = '0, 0\n5, 10\n10, 30';
    fixture.cores[0].records = [{ id: 'record', name: 'Synthetic Cu', axis: 'depth',
      series: [{ name: 'Cu', x: [0, 5, 10], y: [2, 4, 3], show: true, color: '#663399' }] }];
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' }); await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(async (fixture) => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 8; canvas.height = 100;
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d'); context.fillStyle = '#b4b4b4'; context.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 100);
      const blob = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve));
      for (const core of fixture.cores) for (const section of core.sections) section.file = { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', blob };
      const I = window.ichantool; await I.loadProject(fixture); document.getElementById('home').hidden = true;
      for (const core of I.S.cores) for (const section of core.sections) I.analyze(section);
      I.S.fig.type = 'log'; I.S.fig.coreH = 500; I.setMode('fig');
    }, fixture);
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut svg');
    assert.match(await page.$eval('#figOut svg', (svg) => svg.textContent), /Copper/);
    const original = await globals(), firstTab = await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTab);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views == null), true);
    await change('[data-ftrack="image"]', true);
    await change('[data-fview="axis"]', 'age');
    await change('[data-fimage="mask"]', true);
    await change('[data-fview="height"]', 600);
    await change('[data-fview="hideExcl"]', true);
    await change('[data-fview="overlayAlpha"]', 0.7);
    await change('[data-figure-style] [data-st="bgColor"]', '#fff8e8');
    await change('[data-figure-style] [data-axw="total"]', 95);
    await taxa('abundance', 'kite'); await taxa('occurrence', 'names'); await taxa('source', 'marks');
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"] polygon');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut image')?.getAttribute('href')?.startsWith('data:image/png'));
    const pixel = await page.$eval('#figOut image', async (image) => {
      const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(await (await fetch(image.getAttribute('href'))).blob());
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = bitmap.width; canvas.height = bitmap.height;
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d'); context.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
      return [...context.getImageData(Math.floor(bitmap.width / 4), Math.floor(bitmap.height * 4 / 30), 1, 1).data];
    });
    assert.deepEqual(pixel, [18, 102, 130, 255], 'Age-aligned mask pixels use the inverse nonlinear model');
    const mark = await page.$eval('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"][data-taxon="Chondrites"] text', (text) => {
      const clip = document.querySelector('#figOut clipPath[id$="plot"] rect');
      return (+text.getAttribute('y') - 3 - +clip.getAttribute('y')) / +clip.getAttribute('height');
    });
    assert.ok(Math.abs(mark - 2 / 3) < 0.0001);
    await change('[data-colord="figure"] [data-colshow="rec:Cu"]', false);
    await change('[data-fview="from"]', 2); await change('[data-fview="to"]', 28);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => /2.28 ka/.test(document.querySelector('#figOut svg')?.textContent) && !document.querySelector('#figOut svg')?.textContent.includes('Copper'));
    if (screenshots) await page.screenshot({ path: 'dist/figure-composition-log.png' });
    assert.equal(await globals(), original);
    await type('core');
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('[data-fview="axis"]', (input) => input.value), 'depth');
    await change('[data-fview="axis"]', 'age');
    await change('[data-figure-style] [data-st="bgColor"]', '#eef4ee');
    await change('[data-ftrack="BI"]', true);
    await change('[data-ftrack="image"]', true);
    await change('[data-corep="bw"]', true);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut svg')?.textContent.includes('(ka)') && document.querySelector('#figOut image')?.getAttribute('href')?.startsWith('data:image/png'));
    await change('#figKeepRatio', true);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#figCoreH', (input) => input.disabled), true);
    await change('#figKeepRatio', false);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#figCoreH', (input) => input.disabled), false);
    await type('range'); await change('[data-figure-style] [data-st="bgColor"]', '#f0f0ff');
    await change('#figRangeAxis', 'age');
    await type('sections'); await change('[data-figure-style] [data-st="yStep"]', 2);
    await change('#figSecFrom', 1);
    await type('log');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.log.style.bgColor), '#fff8e8');
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('[data-fview="from"]', (input) => input.value), '2');
    assert.equal(await globals(), original);
    await click('#figTabs [data-ftabadd]');
    const secondTab = await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTab);
    await change('[data-figure-style] [data-st="bgColor"]', '#ffdddd');
    await click(`[data-ftab="${firstTab}"] .ftl`);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.log.style.bgColor), '#fff8e8');
    const saved = await page.evaluate(async () => { await window.ichantool.saveToDB(); return window.ichantool.serialize(); });
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.evaluate(async (project) => { const I = window.ichantool; await I.loadProject(project); document.getElementById('home').hidden = true; I.setMode('fig'); }, saved);
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"] polygon');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.log.axis), 'age');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.core.style.bgColor), '#eef4ee');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.sections.style.yStep), 2);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.sections.depthFrom), 1);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      window.__compositionExports = []; const createURL = URL.createObjectURL;
      URL.createObjectURL = (blob) => { if (['image/svg+xml', 'image/png'].includes(blob.type)) window.__compositionExports.push(blob); return createURL(blob); };
      document.addEventListener('click', (event) => { if (event.target.closest?.('a[download]')) event.preventDefault(); }, true);
    });
    await click('#figSvg'); await click('#figPng');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__compositionExports.some((blob) => blob.type === 'image/png'));
    const exported = await page.evaluate(async () => ({ svg: await window.__compositionExports.find((b) => b.type === 'image/svg+xml').text(),
      png: [...new Uint8Array(await window.__compositionExports.find((b) => b.type === 'image/png').arrayBuffer()).slice(0, 8)] }));
    assert.match(exported.svg, /data-role="abundance"/); assert.match(exported.svg, /#fff8e8/);
    assert.deepEqual(exported.png, [137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]);
    await click('#figCompositionReset');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.log == null), true);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('[data-fview="axis"]', (input) => input.value), 'depth');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.core.style.bgColor), '#eef4ee');
    await click(`[data-ftab="${secondTab}"] .ftl`);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.views.log.style.bgColor), '#ffdddd');
    assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    console.log('Figure composition: independent types/tabs, read-only analysis views, nonlinear age images/marks, tracks, styles, save/reload, reset and exports verified.');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Figure composition errors:', errors);
    console.error('Figure composition state:', await page.evaluate(() => JSON.stringify({ figure: window.ichantool.S.fig, output: document.getElementById('figOut').textContent.slice(0, 500) })));
    throw error;
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/first-run.mjs
##########################################

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { mkdir, writeFile, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';

/** Each path starts with a fresh library; the normal UI must be able to leave the uninitialised workspace. */
export async function verifyFirstRun(browser, url) {
  const fixture = resolve('dist', `first-import-${randomUUID()}.sedcoretool`), errors = [];
  await mkdir(resolve('dist'), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(fixture, JSON.stringify({ app: 'SedCoreTool', kind: 'project', version: 5, id: 'first-import', name: 'First imported project',
    cores: [{ id: 'first-core', name: 'First core', units: 'cm', sections: [] }] }));
  try {
    for (const action of ['create', 'import']) {
      const context = await browser.createBrowserContext(), page = await context.newPage();
      page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
      try {
        await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' }); await page.waitForSelector('#projList .pempty');
        assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(() => ({ id: window.ichantool.S.id, cores: window.ichantool.S.cores.length })), { id: '', cores: 0 });
        if (action === 'create') {
          await page.click('#newProjOpen'); await page.type('#newProjName', 'First created project'); await page.click('#newProjBtn');
        } else await (await page.$('#impProj')).uploadFile(fixture);
        await page.waitForFunction((name) => window.ichantool.S.name === name && window.ichantool.S.id
          && document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state === 'saved' && document.getElementById('home').hidden,
        {}, action === 'create' ? 'First created project' : 'First imported project');
        const expected = await page.evaluate(() => ({ id: window.ichantool.S.id, name: window.ichantool.S.name }));
        assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.cores.length), 1);
        await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
        await page.waitForFunction((expected) => window.ichantool?.S.id === expected.id && window.ichantool.S.name === expected.name
          && document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state === 'saved', {}, expected);
      } finally { await context.close(); }
    }
    assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    console.log('First run: real project creation/import from empty libraries and saved reload verified.');
  } finally { await unlink(fixture); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/interrupted-saves.mjs
##########################################

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

/** Exercise real IndexedDB transactions and staged filesystem writes with deterministic failures. */
export async function verifyInterruptedSaves(browser, url) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
  try {
    await page.exposeFunction('reopenInterruptedSave', async (source, expected) => {
      const reader = await browser.newPage();
      reader.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
      try {
        await reader.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
        return await reader.evaluate(async (source, expected) => {
          const I = window.ichantool, storage = await import('./js/storage.js');
          let saved, image;
          if (source === 'database') {
            saved = await storage.getProject(expected.id);
            image = await storage.getImage(saved.cores[0].sections[0].file.key);
          } else {
            const root = await navigator.storage.getDirectory();
            const folder = await (await root.getDirectoryHandle('interrupted-saves-test')).getDirectoryHandle(source);
            saved = JSON.parse(await (await (await folder.getFileHandle('project.json')).getFile()).text());
            const section = saved.cores[0].sections[0], images = await folder.getDirectoryHandle('images');
            image = await (await images.getFileHandle(section.file.path.split('/')[1])).getFile();
            section.file = { name: section.file.name, type: section.file.type, blob: image };
          }
          await I.loadProject(saved);
          const reopened = I.serialize(), section = reopened.cores[0].sections[0], previous = expected.cores[0].sections[0];
          return I.S.id === expected.id && I.S.notes === (expected.notes || '') && section.name === previous.name
            && JSON.stringify(section.labels) === JSON.stringify(previous.labels)
            && JSON.stringify(section.exclude) === JSON.stringify(previous.exclude)
            && image.size === previous.file.size;
        }, source, expected);
      } finally { await reader.close(); }
    });
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    const results = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const I = window.ichantool, storage = await import('./js/storage.js');
      const { markDirty } = await import('./js/app.js');
      const { DEFAULT_FILTERS, DEFAULT_DETECT } = await import('./js/imageproc.js');
      const checks = {}, originalPut = IDBObjectStore.prototype.put, logError = console.error;
      console.error = () => {};
      const pause = () => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 8; canvas.height = 8;
      const image = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve));
      const imageKey = 'interrupted-save-image';
      await storage.putImage(imageKey, image);
      // A stream failure before publishing a new OPFS image must preserve the old reference and bytes.
      let imageRejected = false;
      await storage.putImage(imageKey, {
        size: image.size, type: image.type,
        stream: () => new ReadableStream({ start(controller) { controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array([1, 2])); controller.error(new Error('simulated image interruption')); } }),
      }).catch(() => { imageRejected = true; });
      const originalBytes = Array.from(new Uint8Array(await image.arrayBuffer()));
      checks.interruptedImagePreserved = imageRejected
        && JSON.stringify(Array.from(new Uint8Array(await (await storage.getImage(imageKey)).arrayBuffer()))) === JSON.stringify(originalBytes);
      const fixture = {
        app: 'SedCoreTool', kind: 'project', version: 5, id: 'interrupted-save', name: 'Interrupted save',
        cores: [{ id: 'core', name: 'Synthetic core', sections: [{
          id: 'section', name: 'Last good section', w: 8, h: 8, ds: 1,
          file: { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', key: imageKey, size: image.size },
          top: 0, bottom: 8, units: 'cm', roi: { x0: 0, y0: 0, x1: 8, y1: 8 },
          filters: DEFAULT_FILTERS, detect: DEFAULT_DETECT, nextId: 1, objects: [], labels: [0, 64], exclude: [0, 64],
        }] }],
      };
      await I.loadProject(fixture); markDirty(); await I.saveToDB();
      const good = await storage.getProject(fixture.id), meta = await storage.getMeta(fixture.id);
      const sameGood = async () => JSON.stringify(await storage.getProject(fixture.id)) === JSON.stringify(good)
        && JSON.stringify(await storage.getMeta(fixture.id)) === JSON.stringify(meta);
      const editPendingInterpretation = () => {
        const section = I.S.sections[0], exclude = section.exclude.slice();
        exclude[7] = 1; section.exclude = exclude;
        section.name = 'Pending section'; I.S.notes = 'Pending interpretation'; markDirty();
      };
      try {
        // A synchronous clone failure occurs after meta and data have already been queued.
        const invalid = structuredClone(good);
        invalid.cores[0].sections[0].name = 'Must not commit';
        invalid.cores[0].sections[0].uncloneable = () => {};
        let rejected = false;
        await storage.putProject({ ...meta, name: 'Must not commit' }, invalid).catch(() => { rejected = true; });
        checks.cloneFailureRollsBack = rejected && await sameGood();
        // Restore the baseline so the remaining cases are independent of this result.
        await storage.putProject(meta, good);

        editPendingInterpretation();
        let aborts = 0;
        IDBObjectStore.prototype.put = function (...args) {
          const request = originalPut.apply(this, args);
          if (this.name === 'secs') request.addEventListener('success', () => { aborts++; this.transaction.abort(); });
          return request;
        };
        await I.saveToDB();
        checks.lateAbortRollsBack = aborts === 1 && await sameGood();
        checks.databaseFailureVisible = document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state === 'error';
        checks.databaseEditsPending = I.S.notes === 'Pending interpretation' && I.S.sections[0].name === 'Pending section' && I.S.sections[0].exclude[7] === 1;
        checks.lastDatabaseSaveReopens = await window.reopenInterruptedSave('database', good);

        // Creating another project must not discard the failed project's live edits.
        await I.showHome();
        document.getElementById('newProjOpen').click();
        document.getElementById('newProjName').value = 'Must wait for save';
        document.getElementById('newProjBtn').click(); await pause();
        checks.failedSaveBlocksSwitch = I.S.id === fixture.id && I.S.notes === 'Pending interpretation';
        // A failed replacement import must not mark the still-open interpretation as saved.
        const confirm = window.confirm; window.confirm = () => true;
        const input = document.getElementById('impProj'), transfer = new DataTransfer();
        transfer.items.add(new File([JSON.stringify(good)], 'replacement.json', { type: 'application/json' }));
        const oldToast = document.getElementById('toast').textContent;
        input.files = transfer.files; input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        for (let i = 0; i < 200 && document.getElementById('toast').textContent === oldToast; i++) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
        const leaving = new Event('beforeunload', { cancelable: true }); window.dispatchEvent(leaving);
        checks.failedImportKeepsPending = leaving.defaultPrevented && I.S.notes === 'Pending interpretation' && I.S.sections[0].exclude[7] === 1;
        window.confirm = confirm;
        IDBObjectStore.prototype.put = originalPut;
        // Keep the other cases runnable on the broken implementation too.
        if (I.S.id !== fixture.id) { await I.loadProject(good); editPendingInterpretation(); }
        document.getElementById('saveRetry').click();
        for (let i = 0; i < 200 && document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state !== 'saved'; i++) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
        const retried = await storage.getProject(fixture.id);
        checks.databaseRetry = retried.notes === 'Pending interpretation' && retried.cores[0].sections[0].name === 'Pending section';
        await I.loadProject(retried);
        checks.databaseReopens = I.S.notes === retried.notes && I.S.sections[0].name === 'Pending section' && I.S.sections[0].exclude[7] === 1;

        // Real OPFS writes use the same File System Access writable/commit contract as a picked folder.
        const root = await navigator.storage.getDirectory();
        const folderName = 'interrupted-saves-test';
        await root.removeEntry(folderName, { recursive: true }).catch(() => {});
        const folder = await root.getDirectoryHandle(folderName, { create: true });
        let failure = null, aborted = 0;
        const streams = [];
        const wrapDirectory = (dir) => ({
          name: dir.name, kind: dir.kind, queryPermission: async () => 'granted', requestPermission: async () => 'granted',
          getDirectoryHandle: async (...args) => wrapDirectory(await dir.getDirectoryHandle(...args)),
          removeEntry: (...args) => dir.removeEntry(...args), entries: () => dir.entries(),
          async getFileHandle(name, options) {
            const handle = await dir.getFileHandle(name, options);
            return {
              getFile: () => handle.getFile(),
              async createWritable() {
                if (name === failure?.name && failure.stage === 'open') throw new Error('simulated open failure');
                const stream = await handle.createWritable(); streams.push(stream);
                const write = stream.write.bind(stream), close = stream.close.bind(stream), abort = stream.abort.bind(stream);
                stream.write = async (chunk) => {
                  await write(chunk);
                  if (name === failure?.name && failure.stage === 'write') throw new Error('simulated write failure');
                };
                stream.close = async () => {
                  if (name === failure?.name && failure.stage === 'close') throw new Error('simulated close failure');
                  await close();
                };
                stream.abort = async (reason) => { aborted++; await abort(reason); };
                return stream;
              },
            };
          },
        });
        const picker = window.showDirectoryPicker;
        window.showDirectoryPicker = async () => wrapDirectory(folder);
        const chooseBackup = async () => {
          document.getElementById('bkStatus').dataset.state = 'running';
          document.getElementById('bkChoose').click();
          for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
            if (document.getElementById('bkStatus').dataset.state === (failure ? 'error' : 'ok')) return;
          }
          throw new Error('backup status timeout');
        };
        try {
          await chooseBackup();
          const projectDir = (await Array.fromAsync(folder.values())).find((entry) => entry.kind === 'directory' && entry.name.endsWith('__' + fixture.id));
          const readManifest = async () => (await (await projectDir.getFileHandle('project.json')).getFile()).text();
          const manifest = await readManifest();
          const saved = JSON.parse(manifest), path = saved.cores[0].sections[0].file.path;
          const imageDir = await projectDir.getDirectoryHandle('images');
          checks.folderImageReadable = (await (await imageDir.getFileHandle(path.split('/')[1])).getFile()).size === image.size;
          for (const stage of ['open', 'write', 'close']) {
            const previousAborts = aborted;
            I.S.notes = 'Folder pending ' + stage; markDirty(); await I.saveToDB();
            failure = { name: 'project.json', stage };
            await chooseBackup();
            checks['folderPreserved_' + stage] = await readManifest() === manifest;
            checks['folderVisible_' + stage] = document.getElementById('bkStatus').dataset.state === 'error';
            checks['folderPending_' + stage] = I.S.notes === 'Folder pending ' + stage;
            checks['folderRetryOffered_' + stage] = !document.getElementById('bkReactivate').hidden;
            checks['lastFolderSaveReopens_' + stage] = await window.reopenInterruptedSave(projectDir.name, retried);
            if (stage !== 'open') checks['folderWriterAborted_' + stage] = aborted > previousAborts;
          }
          // Replacing an image must not change the image referenced by the last good manifest.
          canvas.getContext('2d').fillRect(0, 0, 4, 4);
          const replacement = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve));
          await storage.putImage('replacement-image', replacement);
          I.S.sections[0].file = { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', key: 'replacement-image', size: replacement.size };
          markDirty(); await I.saveToDB();
          await chooseBackup();
          checks.folderPreviousImagePreserved = JSON.stringify(Array.from(new Uint8Array(await (await (await imageDir.getFileHandle(path.split('/')[1])).getFile()).arrayBuffer()))) === JSON.stringify(originalBytes);
          checks.folderPreviousManifestPreserved = await readManifest() === manifest;
          checks.folderReopensAfterImageFailure = await window.reopenInterruptedSave(projectDir.name, retried);
          const latest = await storage.getProject(fixture.id);
          failure = null;
          // Retry through the user-facing control, without another edit or folder selection.
          document.getElementById('bkReactivate').click();
          for (let i = 0; i < 200 && document.getElementById('bkStatus').dataset.state !== 'ok'; i++) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
          const recovered = JSON.parse(await readManifest());
          checks.folderRetry = recovered.notes === 'Folder pending close' && document.getElementById('bkStatus').dataset.state === 'ok';
          checks.folderReopens = await window.reopenInterruptedSave(projectDir.name, latest);
        } finally {
          window.showDirectoryPicker = picker;
          for (const stream of streams) await stream.abort().catch(() => {});
          await root.removeEntry(folderName, { recursive: true });
        }
      } finally { IDBObjectStore.prototype.put = originalPut; console.error = logError; }
      return checks;
    });
    for (const [name, passed] of Object.entries(results)) assert.equal(passed, true, name + ': ' + JSON.stringify(results));
    assert.deepEqual(errors, [], 'interrupted saves produce no uncaught page errors');
    console.log('Interrupted saves: IndexedDB rollback, folder preservation, visible failure and retry verified.');
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/multiproxy-figure.mjs
