import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { taxonFigureFixture } from './taxon-figures.mjs';

export function multiproxyFigureFixture() {
  const fixture = taxonFigureFixture(), first = fixture.cores[0];
  first.imp = { depth: [0, 5, 10], proxies: { Ca: [1, 3, 2], Ti: [4, 2, 5] } };
  const second = structuredClone(first);
  second.id = 'other-core'; second.name = 'Other core'; second.units = 'm';
  second.age.text = '1, 100\n2, 200';
  second.imp = { depth: [1, 1.5, 2], proxies: { Ca: [10, 30, 20], Ti: [40, 20, 50] } };
  second.sections[0].id = 'other-section'; second.sections[0].top = 100; second.sections[0].bottom = 200;
  fixture.cores.push(second, { id: 'numeric-core', name: 'Numeric only', units: 'cm', sections: [],
    imp: { depth: [0, 5, 10], proxies: { Ca: [5, 6, 7] } } });
  fixture.geo = { settings: { axis: 'depth', step: 1, features: ['Ca', 'Ti'] } };
  return fixture;
}

/** Exercise real controls and saved projects in both served and offline builds. */
export async function verifyMultiproxyFigure(browser, url, { screenshots = false } = {}) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
  page.setDefaultTimeout(15000);
  const change = (selector, value) => page.evaluate((selector, value) => {
    const input = document.querySelector(selector);
    if (!input) throw new Error('Missing figure control: ' + selector);
    if (input.type === 'checkbox') input.checked = value; else input.value = String(value);
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
  }, selector, value);
  const click = (selector) => page.evaluate((selector) => document.querySelector(selector).click(), selector);
  const taxa = (key, value) => change(`[data-taxa-scope="figure"] [data-taxa="${key}"]`, value);
  const globalState = () => page.evaluate(() => {
    const S = window.ichantool.S;
    return JSON.stringify({ core: S.curCore, age: S.age.text, analysis: S.geo.settings, view: S.geo.view, log: S.logView,
      data: S.cores.map((c) => ({ imp: c.imp, proxies: c.proxies, sections: c.sections.map((s) => ({
        labels: [...s.labels], exclude: [...s.exclude], marks: s.marks,
        objects: [...s.objects.values()].map((o) => [o.id, o.cls]),
        bins: s.bins })) })) });
  });
  try {
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(async (fixture) => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 8; canvas.height = 100;
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d'); context.fillStyle = '#b4b4b4'; context.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 100);
      const blob = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve));
      for (const core of fixture.cores) for (const section of core.sections) section.file = { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', blob };
      const I = window.ichantool; await I.loadProject(fixture); document.getElementById('home').hidden = true;
      for (const core of I.S.cores) for (const section of core.sections) I.analyze(section);
      I.S.fig.type = 'geolog'; I.setMode('fig');
    }, multiproxyFigureFixture());
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut svg');
    const original = await globalState(), firstTab = await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTab);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.geoView == null), true);
    await taxa('abundance', 'kite'); await taxa('occurrence', 'names'); await taxa('source', 'marks');
    await taxa('opacity', 35); await taxa('scale', 'percent'); await taxa('values', true);
    await change('[data-fgeo-track="image"]', false);
    await change('[data-fgeo-track="BI"]', true);
    await change('[data-fgeo-track="secs"]', true);
    await change('[data-fgeo-field="height"]', 600);
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"] polygon');
    assert.equal(await page.$$eval('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"]', (groups) => groups.length), 2);
    assert.match(await page.$eval('#figOut svg', (svg) => svg.textContent), /50%/);
    const proxyPosition = () => page.$$eval('[data-colord="figure"] [data-colid]', (items) => items.findIndex((item) => item.dataset.colid === 'rec:Ca'));
    const originalProxyPosition = await proxyPosition();
    await click('[data-colord="figure"] [data-colid="rec:Ca"] [data-colmv="-1"]');
    assert.equal(await proxyPosition(), originalProxyPosition - 1);
    await change('[data-colord="figure"] [data-colshow="sp:Planolites"]', false);
    await change('[data-colord="figure"] [data-colshow="rec:Ti"]', false);
    await change('[data-colord="figure"] [data-colshow="band:fam:events"]', false);
    await change('[data-figure-style] [data-axw="sp:Chondrites"]', 110);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => !document.querySelector('#figOut [data-taxon="Planolites"]'));
    assert.equal(await globalState(), original);
    await change('#figGeoCore', 'other-core');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut svg')?.textContent.includes('Other core'));
    const markDepth = await page.$eval('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"][data-taxon="Chondrites"] text', (text) => {
      const clip = document.querySelector('#figOut clipPath[id$="plot"] rect');
      return (+text.getAttribute('y') - 3 - +clip.getAttribute('y')) / +clip.getAttribute('height');
    });
    assert.ok(Math.abs(markDepth - 0.75) < 0.0001, 'Selected core marks use its centimetre-to-metre conversion');
    assert.equal(await globalState(), original);
    if (screenshots) await page.screenshot({ path: 'dist/multiproxy-composition.png' });
    await click('#figTabs [data-ftabadd]');
    await taxa('layout', 'overlay'); await taxa('abundance', 'area'); await taxa('opacity', 80);
    const secondTab = await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTab);
    await change('[data-fgeo-field="from"]', 1.2); await change('[data-fgeo-field="to"]', 1.9);
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"][opacity="0.8"]');
    await click(`[data-ftab="${firstTab}"] .ftl`);
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"][opacity="0.35"] polygon');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.geoView.from == null), true);
    const saved = await page.evaluate(async () => { await window.ichantool.saveToDB(); return window.ichantool.serialize(); });
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.evaluate(async (project) => { const I = window.ichantool; await I.loadProject(project); document.getElementById('home').hidden = true; I.setMode('fig'); }, saved);
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"][opacity="0.35"] polygon');
    assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.geoView.style.colHide), ['sp:Planolites', 'rec:Ti', 'band:fam:events']);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.curCore), 'taxon-core');
    await change('[data-fview="axis"]', 'age');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut svg')?.textContent.includes('(ka)'));
    const agePosition = await page.$eval('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"][data-taxon="Chondrites"] text', (text) => {
      const clip = document.querySelector('#figOut clipPath[id$="plot"] rect');
      return (+text.getAttribute('y') - 3 - +clip.getAttribute('y')) / +clip.getAttribute('height');
    });
    assert.ok(Math.abs(agePosition - 0.75) < 0.0001, 'Selected core uses its own age model');
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      window.__figureExports = []; const createURL = URL.createObjectURL;
      URL.createObjectURL = (blob) => { if (['image/svg+xml', 'image/png'].includes(blob.type)) window.__figureExports.push(blob); return createURL(blob); };
      document.addEventListener('click', (event) => { if (event.target.closest?.('a[download]')) event.preventDefault(); }, true);
    });
    await click('#figSvg'); await click('#figPng');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__figureExports.some((blob) => blob.type === 'image/png'));
    const exports = await page.evaluate(async () => ({ svg: await window.__figureExports.find((b) => b.type === 'image/svg+xml').text(),
      signature: [...new Uint8Array(await window.__figureExports.find((b) => b.type === 'image/png').arrayBuffer()).slice(0, 8)] }));
    assert.match(exports.svg, /data-role="abundance" opacity="0.35"/);
    assert.deepEqual(exports.signature, [137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]);
    await change('[data-fview="axis"]', 'depth');
    await change('#figGeoCore', 'numeric-core');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut svg')?.textContent.includes('Numeric only'));
    assert.equal(await page.$('#figOut [data-role="abundance"]'), null);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('[data-fgeo-track="species"]', (input) => input.disabled), true);
    await click('#figGeoCompositionReset');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.geoView == null && window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles.geolog == null), true);
    await click(`[data-ftab="${secondTab}"] .ftl`);
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles.geolog.opacity), 0.8);
    assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    console.log('Multiproxy figures: taxa/proxies, independent composition, mixed core units, own age model, exact data, tabs, save/reload, reset and SVG/PNG verified.');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Multiproxy figure errors:', errors);
    console.error('Multiproxy figure state:', await page.evaluate(() => JSON.stringify({ fig: window.ichantool.S.fig, output: document.getElementById('figOut').textContent.slice(0, 500) })));
    throw error;
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/navigation-preview-cache.mjs
##########################################

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { taxonFigureFixture } from './taxon-figures.mjs';

/** Keep previews resident while changing their inputs; reference preparation must not clear the preview cache. */
export async function verifyNavigationPreviewCache(browser, url) {
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  try {
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(async (fixture) => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 8; canvas.height = 100;
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
      for (let y = 0; y < 100; y++) { context.fillStyle = `rgb(${y + 50},${y + 80},${200 - y})`; context.fillRect(0, y, 8, 1); }
      fixture.cores[0].sections[0].file = { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', blob: await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve)) };
      await window.ichantool.loadProject(fixture);
      document.getElementById('home').hidden = true;
    }, taxonFigureFixture());
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool.S.sections[0]?.enh);
    const results = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const { navigationPreviewCanvas, sectionCanvas, buildColumnLog, clearNavigationPreviews } = await import('./js/columns.js');
      const I = window.ichantool, s = I.S.sections[0], view = I.S.logView, checks = [];
      let previous = null;
      const verify = (name, width = 24, height = 600) => {
        const preview = navigationPreviewCanvas(s, width, height);
        if (!preview || (previous && previous.width !== 0)) throw new Error(`${name}: invalidated canvas was retained`);
        if (navigationPreviewCanvas(s, width, height) !== preview) throw new Error(`${name}: unchanged preview was rebuilt`);
        s._lc = null; s._lb = null;
        const legacy = sectionCanvas(s), scale = s._lc.k, roi = s.roi;
        const expected = document.createElement('canvas'); expected.width = width; expected.height = height;
        expected.getContext('2d').drawImage(legacy, roi.x0 * scale, roi.y0 * scale, (roi.x1 - roi.x0) * scale, (roi.y1 - roi.y0) * scale, 0, 0, width, height);
        const a = preview.getContext('2d').getImageData(0, 0, width, height).data;
        const b = expected.getContext('2d').getImageData(0, 0, width, height).data;
        for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) throw new Error(`${name}: pixel ${i} differs`);
        previous = preview; checks.push(name);
      };
      verify('initial');
      view.imgMode = view.imgMode === 'orig' ? 'enh' : 'orig'; verify('image source');
      view.overlay = !view.overlay; verify('overlay');
      view.hideExcl = !view.hideExcl; verify('excluded zones');
      I.S.ovAlpha = 0.71; verify('opacity');
      I.S.classes[0].color = '#b32d8a'; verify('class colour');
      I.S.settings.predThr = 0.61; verify('prediction threshold');
      I.S.settings.includePred = !I.S.settings.includePred; verify('prediction inclusion');
      I.S.settings.countCandidates = !I.S.settings.countCandidates; verify('candidate inclusion');
      s.roi = { x0: 1, x1: 7, y0: 5, y1: 95 }; verify('analysis region');
      view.overlay = true; I.S.selected = 1; I.assignClass('pl'); verify('trace assignment');
      verify('preview dimensions', 16, 500);
      for (const [width, height] of [[0, 500], [25, 500], [16, 4097], [8.5, 500], [8, NaN]]) {
        if (navigationPreviewCanvas(s, width, height) !== null) throw new Error('Unsupported dimensions must fall back');
      }
      const roi = s.roi;
      for (const invalid of [null, { ...roi, y1: roi.y0 }, { ...roi, x1: s.w + 1 }]) {
        s.roi = invalid;
        if (navigationPreviewCanvas(s, 16, 500) !== null) throw new Error('Invalid saved regions must use the existing fallback');
      }
      s.roi = roi;
      const checkbox = document.getElementById('navPreview');
      checkbox.checked = false; checkbox.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
      if (previous.width !== 0) throw new Error('Disabling the preview must release its canvases');
      previous = null; verify('rollback');
      view.tracks.image = true;
      const before = buildColumnLog().svg;
      checkbox.checked = true; checkbox.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
      if (buildColumnLog().svg !== before) throw new Error('Navigation flag changed column SVG');
      clearNavigationPreviews();
      if (previous.width !== 0) throw new Error('Project-reset cache clearing must release previews');
      return checks;
    });
    assert.equal(results.length, 13);
    console.log('Navigation preview cache: retained hits, input invalidation, disposal, bounds, rollback and exact column SVG verified.');
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/navigation-preview.mjs

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { objectStats } from '../../app/js/imageproc.js';
import { rleDecode, rleEncode } from '../../app/js/core.js';
import { zipFiles } from '../../app/js/zip.js';
import { taxonFigureFixture } from './taxon-figures.mjs';

/** Reference preparation is confined to a separate correctness trial, never to a timed/RSS trial. */
export async function compareNavigationPreview(page) {
  return page.evaluate(async () => {
    const frame = () => new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(resolve)));
    await document.fonts.ready;
    const checkbox = document.getElementById('navPreview'), view = document.getElementById('view');
    const context = view.getContext('2d'), before = checkbox.checked;
    const select = async (enabled) => { checkbox.checked = enabled; checkbox.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); await frame(); await frame(); };
    await select(false);
    const reference = context.getImageData(0, 0, 66, view.height).data;
    await select(true);
    const actual = context.getImageData(0, 0, 66, view.height).data;
    if (reference.length !== actual.length) throw new Error('Navigation geometry changed');
    for (let i = 0; i < actual.length; i++) if (actual[i] !== reference[i]) throw new Error(`Navigation pixel ${i}: expected ${reference[i]}, received ${actual[i]}`);
    await select(before);
    return { width: 66, height: view.height, exact_pixels: reference.length / 4 };
  });
}

export async function verifyNavigationPreview(browser, url, { screenshots = false } = {}) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.setDefaultTimeout(20000); page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message)); page.on('dialog', (dialog) => dialog.accept());
  const change = (selector, value, event = 'change') => page.evaluate((selector, value, event) => {
    const field = document.querySelector(selector); if (field.type === 'checkbox') field.checked = value; else field.value = String(value);
    field.dispatchEvent(new Event(event, { bubbles: true }));
  }, selector, value, event);
  const frame = () => page.evaluate(() => new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(resolve))));
  const science = () => page.evaluate(() => {
    const I = window.ichantool;
    return JSON.stringify(I.S.sections.map((s) => { I.analyze(s); return { labels: s._mk.labels.rle(), exclude: s._mk.exclude.rle(),
      objects: [...s.objects.values()].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id).map((o) => [o.id, o.cls]), marks: s.marks, bins: s.bins }; }));
  });
  try {
    const fixture = taxonFigureFixture(), section = fixture.cores[0].sections[0], originalLabels = rleDecode(section.labels, section.w * section.h, Int32Array);
    const labels = new Int32Array(80 * section.h), rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(labels.length * 4);
    for (let y = 0; y < section.h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 80; x++) {
      const i = y * 80 + x; labels[i] = originalLabels[y * 8 + Math.floor(x / 10)]; rgba.set([y + 60, y + 90, 200 - y, 255], i * 4);
    }
    section.w = 80; section.roi.x1 = 80; section.labels = rleEncode(labels);
    const statistics = objectStats(labels, section.w, section.h, rgba);
    for (const object of section.objects) object.st = statistics.get(object.id);
    for (const mark of section.marks) mark.x *= 10;
    const exclude = new Uint8Array(section.w * section.h);
    exclude.fill(1, 30 * section.w, 33 * section.w); exclude[55 * section.w + 3] = 1; section.exclude = rleEncode(exclude);
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' }); await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(async (fixture) => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 80; canvas.height = 100;
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
      for (let y = 0; y < 100; y++) { context.fillStyle = `rgb(${y + 60},${y + 90},${200 - y})`; context.fillRect(0, y, 80, 1); }
      fixture.cores[0].sections[0].file = { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', blob: await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve)) };
      const I = window.ichantool; await I.loadProject(fixture); document.getElementById('home').hidden = true; I.setMode('section');
      await I.saveToDB();
    }, fixture);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool.S.sections[0]?.enh);
    await frame(); const original = await science();
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#navPreview', (checkbox) => checkbox.checked), true);
    await compareNavigationPreview(page);
    assert.equal(await science(), original);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#saveState', (status) => status.dataset.state), 'saved');
    for (const source of ['orig', 'enh']) for (const overlay of [false, true]) for (const hidden of [false, true]) {
      await change('#logImgMode', source); await change('#logOverlay', overlay); await change('#logHideExcl', hidden);
      for (const opacity of [35, 65, 100]) { await change('#ovAlpha', opacity, 'input'); await compareNavigationPreview(page); }
    }
    assert.equal(await science(), original);
    await change('#navPreview', true);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      const I = window.ichantool; I.S.selected = 1; I.assignClass('pl');
      I.S.classes[0].color = '#b32d8a'; const s = I.S.sections[0]; s.roi = { x0: 1, x1: 7, y0: 5, y1: 95 }; s.logVer++;
      I.setMode('section');
    });
    await compareNavigationPreview(page);
    await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 800 }); await compareNavigationPreview(page);
    await page.setViewport({ width: 1440, height: 900 }); await compareNavigationPreview(page);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      const I = window.ichantool; I.S.view.zoom = 8; I.setMode('section');
      document.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => { if (event.target.id === 'view') window.__navPoint = event.clientY; }, true);
      document.addEventListener('pointermove', (event) => { if (event.target.id === 'view') window.__navPoint = event.clientY; }, true);
    });
    await frame(); const beforePointer = await science(), bounds = await page.$eval('#view', (canvas) => {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: rect.left, y: rect.top, height: canvas.height };
    });
    const checkPosition = async () => assert.ok(await page.evaluate(() => {
      const { S } = window.ichantool, view = document.getElementById('view'), section = S.sections[0], top = view.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      const fraction = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (window.__navPoint - top - 14) / (view.height - 30)));
      const expected = section.roi.y0 + fraction * (section.roi.y1 - section.roi.y0);
      return Math.abs(S.view.y + view.height / (2 * S.view.zoom) - expected) <= 1e-9;
    }), 'Navigation click/drag must preserve depth mapping');
    const x = Math.round(bounds.x + 16), start = Math.round(bounds.y + 14 + (bounds.height - 30) * 0.4), end = Math.round(bounds.y + 14 + (bounds.height - 30) * 0.7);
    await page.mouse.click(x, start); await checkPosition();
    await page.mouse.move(x, start); await page.mouse.down(); await page.mouse.move(x, end, { steps: 3 }); await page.mouse.up(); await checkPosition();
    assert.equal(await science(), beforePointer);
    await page.select('#secRes', '2');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool.S.sections[0].ds === 2 && !window.ichantool.S.sections[0]._loading && document.getElementById('busy').hidden);
    await compareNavigationPreview(page);
    if (screenshots) await page.screenshot({ path: 'dist/navigation-preview.png' });
    const saved = await page.evaluate(async () => { await window.ichantool.saveToDB(); return window.ichantool.serialize(); });
    assert.equal(JSON.stringify(saved).includes('navPreview'), false);
    await change('#navPreview', false);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#navPreview', (checkbox) => checkbox.checked), false);
    await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.saveToDB());
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool?.S.sections[0]?.enh);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#navPreview', (checkbox) => checkbox.checked), true);
    await compareNavigationPreview(page);
    assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    console.log('Navigation preview: exact display pixels, source/overlay/exclusion/opacity, edits, ROI, resize, resolution, pointer depth, rollback and session-only state verified.');
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}

/** Older project formats enter through the ordinary importer, with real inline image bytes and masks. */
export async function verifyLegacyNavigation(browser, url) {
  for (const version of [2, 3]) {
    const context = await browser.createBrowserContext(), page = await context.newPage(), errors = [];
    page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
    try {
      await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
      await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
      const fixture = taxonFigureFixture(), core = fixture.cores[0], section = core.sections[0];
      section.file = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 8; canvas.height = 100;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); ctx.fillStyle = '#b4b4b4'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 100);
        return { name: 'legacy.png', type: 'image/png', b64: canvas.toDataURL().split(',')[1] };
      });
      fixture.version = version; fixture.id = `navigation-v${version}`; fixture.app = version === 2 ? 'IchAnTool' : 'CorAnTool';
      if (version === 2) {
        Object.assign(fixture, { sections: core.sections, age: core.age, logView: core.logView });
        delete fixture.cores;
      }
      const json = JSON.stringify(fixture), file = version === 2 ? new Blob([json]) : await zipFiles([{ name: 'project.json', data: json }]);
      await page.evaluate((bytes, name) => {
        const input = document.getElementById('impProj'), transfer = new DataTransfer();
        transfer.items.add(new File([new Uint8Array(bytes)], name)); input.files = transfer.files;
        input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
      }, [...new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer())], version === 2 ? 'navigation-v2.json' : 'navigation-v3.corantool');
      await page.waitForFunction((id) => window.ichantool.S.id === id && window.ichantool.S.sections[0]?.enh
        && document.getElementById('busy').hidden && document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state === 'saved', {}, fixture.id);
      const snapshot = () => page.evaluate(() => {
        const I = window.ichantool, section = I.S.sections[0]; I.analyze(section);
        return { labels: section._mk.labels.rle(), exclude: section._mk.exclude.rle(), bins: section.bins,
          objects: [...section.objects.values()].map((o) => [o.id, o.cls]) };
      });
      const before = await snapshot();
      assert.deepEqual(before.labels, section.labels); assert.deepEqual(before.exclude, section.exclude);
      assert.equal(await page.$eval('#navPreview', (checkbox) => checkbox.checked), true);
      await compareNavigationPreview(page); assert.deepEqual(await snapshot(), before);
      await page.$eval('#navPreview', (checkbox) => checkbox.click());
      await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.saveToDB());
      assert.equal(JSON.stringify(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.serialize())).includes('navPreview'), false);
      await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
      await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool?.S.sections[0]?.enh);
      assert.equal(await page.$eval('#navPreview', (checkbox) => checkbox.checked), true);
      assert.deepEqual(await snapshot(), before); await compareNavigationPreview(page);
      assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    } finally { await context.close(); }
  }
  console.log('Navigation defaults: IchAnTool v2 JSON and CorAnTool v3 package import, exact masks/results, session rollback and migrated reload verified.');
}

ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/smoke.mjs

// V0.42: end-to-end check in a headless Chrome. Needs the repository served (python3 -m http.server 8765) and
// puppeteer-core (npm i --no-save puppeteer-core@23). CHROME = path of Chrome (default /usr/bin/google-chrome).
// Opens the IODP test core, detects, undoes, saves, reloads and compares; fails on any page error.
// V0.49: also compares the web analysis of the idealized core (browser JPEG decoder) with the V2.1 reference CSV.
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { verifyInterruptedSaves } from './interrupted-saves.mjs';
import { verifyNavigationPreview, verifyLegacyNavigation } from './navigation-preview.mjs';
import { verifyNavigationPreviewCache } from './navigation-preview-cache.mjs';
import { verifyFirstRun } from './first-run.mjs';
import { verifyDataTable } from './data-table.mjs';
import { verifyTableViewport } from './table-viewport.mjs';
import { verifyTaxonFigures } from './taxon-figures.mjs';
import { verifyMultiproxyFigure } from './multiproxy-figure.mjs';
import { verifyFigureComposition } from './figure-composition.mjs';
const { default: puppeteer } = await import(process.env.PUPPETEER_MODULE || 'puppeteer-core');

const URL = process.env.APP_URL || 'http://localhost:8765/app/index.html';
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME || '/usr/bin/google-chrome', headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'], defaultViewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
const p = await b.newPage(), errs = [];
p.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
p.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/404|Failed to load resource|simulated quota exceeded/.test(m.text())) errs.push(m.text()); });
p.on('dialog', (d) => d.accept());
const W = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const until = async (fn, ms = 60000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { if (await p.evaluate(fn)) return; await W(500); } throw new Error('timeout: ' + fn); };
const fail = (m) => { console.error('E2E FAIL:', m); process.exitCode = 1; };
try {
  await verifyFirstRun(b, URL);
  await verifyNavigationPreview(b, URL);
  await verifyLegacyNavigation(b, URL);
  await verifyNavigationPreviewCache(b, URL);
  await verifyInterruptedSaves(b, URL);
  await verifyDataTable(b, URL);
  await verifyTableViewport(b, URL);
  await verifyTaxonFigures(b, URL);
  await verifyMultiproxyFigure(b, URL);
  await verifyFigureComposition(b, URL);
  await p.goto(URL, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('openTest').click());
  await until(() => window.ichantool?.S.sections.length === 6 && window.ichantool.S.sections.every((s) => s.objects.size > 0));
  await until(() => document.getElementById('saveState').dataset.state === 'saved');
  // The column controls change only the drawing and can restore their defaults.
  const ui = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const I = window.ichantool; I.setMode('log');
    const mark = document.querySelector('#logTracks [data-track="marks"]');
    const initial = mark.checked; mark.checked = false; mark.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
    const hidden = I.S.logView.tracks.marks === false;
    const color = document.querySelector('#logStyle [data-st="biColor"]');
    color.value = '#123456'; color.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 300));
    const applied = document.querySelector('#logSvg svg')?.outerHTML.includes('#123456');
    document.querySelector('#logStyle [data-colreset="log"]').click();
    const reset = I.S.logView.style.biColor == null;
    document.querySelector('#logTracks [data-track="marks"]').click(); I.setMode('section');
    return { initial, hidden, applied, reset };
  });
  if (!Object.values(ui).every(Boolean)) fail(`column controls: ${JSON.stringify(ui)}`);
  // A drawing failure must invalidate the previously exportable figure.
  const figureFailure = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const I = window.ichantool, figures = await import('./js/figures.js');
    I.setMode('log'); I.setMode('fig'); I.S.fig.type = 'log'; figures.renderFigure();
    const hadFigure = !!document.querySelector('#figOut svg');
    const fig = I.S.fig, previous = fig.type, logError = console.error, createURL = URL.createObjectURL;
    let exports = 0; URL.createObjectURL = (...args) => { exports++; return createURL(...args); };
    console.error = () => {};
    try {
      Object.defineProperty(fig, 'type', { configurable: true, get() { throw new Error('simulated drawing failure'); } });
      figures.renderFigure();
      document.getElementById('figSvg').click();
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
      return { hadFigure, errorShown: document.getElementById('figOut').textContent.includes('simulated drawing failure'), exports };
    } finally {
      Object.defineProperty(fig, 'type', { configurable: true, writable: true, enumerable: true, value: previous });
      URL.createObjectURL = createURL; console.error = logError; I.setMode('section');
    }
  });
  if (!figureFailure.hadFigure || !figureFailure.errorShown || figureFailure.exports) fail(`stale figure export: ${JSON.stringify(figureFailure)}`);
  const first = await p.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.sections[window.ichantool.S.cur].objects.size);
  const sectionMask = () => {
    const { S, serialize } = window.ichantool;
    const section = serialize().cores.find((core) => core.id === S.curCore).sections[S.cur];
    return { nextId: section.nextId, labels: section.labels, stacks: section.stacks,
      objects: section.objects.map(({ id, cls, src, st }) => ({ id, cls, src, st })) };
  };
  const beforeDetection = await p.evaluate(sectionMask);
  await p.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.runDetection()); await W(3000);
  const again = await p.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.sections[window.ichantool.S.cur].objects.size);
  if (again !== first) fail(`detection is not repeatable: ${first} → ${again}`);
  const detected = await p.evaluate(sectionMask);
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('undoBtn').click());
  assert.deepEqual(await p.evaluate(sectionMask), beforeDetection, 'undo restores detection masks, IDs and statistics');
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('redoBtn').click());
  assert.deepEqual(await p.evaluate(sectionMask), detected, 'redo restores detection masks, IDs and statistics');
  // one undoable edit: delete a trace, then undo
  const del = await p.evaluate(() => { const I = window.ichantool, s = I.S.sections[I.S.cur], id = [...s.objects.keys()][0]; I.S.selected = id; document.body.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Delete', bubbles: true })); return [s.objects.size, s.objects.has(id), id]; });
  if (del[1]) fail('delete did not remove the trace');
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('undoBtn').click()); await W(500);
  const undone = await p.evaluate((id) => window.ichantool.S.sections[window.ichantool.S.cur].objects.has(id), del[2]);
  if (!undone) fail('undo did not restore the trace');
  // Fill the stack table, then delete a trace whose overlap will only be needed by undo.
  const compacted = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const { idsAt } = await import('./js/core.js');
    const I = window.ichantool, s = I.S.sections[I.S.cur], [firstId, secondId] = s.objects.keys();
    const pixel = s.labels.indexOf(firstId);
    const shared = [secondId, firstId];
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) s.stacks.push([s.nextId + 2 * i, s.nextId + 2 * i + 1]);
    s.stacks.push(shared);
    s.labels[pixel] = 1 - s.stacks.length;
    s._six = null;
    I.S.selected = firstId;
    document.body.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Delete', bubbles: true }));
    const reclaimed = s.labels[pixel] === secondId && s.stacks.length < 64 && !s.stacks.includes(shared);
    document.getElementById('undoBtn').click();
    const restored = JSON.stringify(idsAt(s.labels[pixel], s.stacks)) === JSON.stringify(shared);
    document.getElementById('redoBtn').click();
    const deletedAgain = s.labels[pixel] === secondId && !s.objects.has(firstId);
    document.getElementById('undoBtn').click();
    const restoredAgain = JSON.stringify(idsAt(s.labels[pixel], s.stacks)) === JSON.stringify(shared);
    s.labels[pixel] = firstId;
    return [reclaimed, restored, deletedAgain, restoredAgain];
  });
  assert.deepEqual(compacted, [true, true, true, true], 'undo and redo preserve overlap order across compaction');

  const exclusion = () => {
    const { S, serialize } = window.ichantool;
    return serialize().cores.find((core) => core.id === S.curCore).sections[S.cur].exclude;
  };
  const beforeExclude = await p.evaluate(exclusion);
  await until(() => !!window.ichantool.S.sections[window.ichantool.S.cur].orig);
  const point = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const { S, setTool } = window.ichantool, s = S.sections[S.cur];
    setTool('exclude'); S.exShape = 'brush';
    await new Promise(requestAnimationFrame);
    const canvas = document.getElementById('view');
    const { left, top, width, height } = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = left + width / 2, y = top + height / 2;
    const hit = document.elementFromPoint(x, y);
    if (hit !== canvas) throw new Error(`Exclusion brush target covered by ${hit?.id || hit?.tagName}`);
    S.view = { zoom: 1, x: s.roi.x0 + 5.5 - width / 2, y: s.roi.y0 + 5.5 - height / 2 };
    return { x, y };
  });
  await p.mouse.click(point.x, point.y);
  const afterExclude = await p.evaluate(exclusion);
  assert.notDeepEqual(afterExclude, beforeExclude, 'exclusion brush changes the mask');
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('undoBtn').click());
  assert.deepEqual(await p.evaluate(exclusion), beforeExclude, 'undo restores the previous exclusion mask');
  await p.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('redoBtn').click());
  assert.deepEqual(await p.evaluate(exclusion), afterExclude, 'redo restores the edited exclusion mask');
  await p.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.saveToDB()); await W(1500);
  const sig = () => window.ichantool.S.sections.map((s) => `${s.objects.size}:${s.labels.reduce((a, v) => a + (v ? 1 : 0), 0)}`).join(',');
  const before = await p.evaluate(sig);
  await p.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
  await until(() => window.ichantool?.S.sections.length === 6);
  await p.evaluate(async () => { for (const s of window.ichantool.S.sections) void s.labels; });
  const after = await p.evaluate(sig);

  // V0.49: web–V2 comparison with the browser's own JPEG decoder (L < 6, rowCounts, binRows of 150 px). The decoder
  // differs from Pillow's at the edges of the painted traces, so percentages may differ slightly; BI and ii must not.
  const v2 = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const { rowCounts, binRows } = await import('./js/core.js'), { parseTable } = await import('./js/log.js');
    const img = new Image(); img.src = '../examples/data/idealized_core_0-500cm.jpg'; await img.decode();
    const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height; const x = c.getContext('2d'); x.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
    const d = x.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data, labels = new Int32Array(c.width * c.height);
    for (let i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) labels[i] = 0.299 * d[4 * i] + 0.587 * d[4 * i + 1] + 0.114 * d[4 * i + 2] < 6 ? 1 : 0;
    const bins = binRows(rowCounts({ labels, width: c.width, roi: { x0: 0, x1: c.width, y0: 0, y1: c.height }, exclude: null, effClass: [-1, 0], nClasses: 1 }), { top: 0, bottom: 500, binPx: 150 });
    const t = parseTable(await (await fetch('../legacy/v2.1_console/example_output.csv')).text()), k = (n) => t.headers.indexOf(n);
    const dp = t.rows.map((r, i) => Math.abs(bins[i].pct - r[k('Bioturbation Percentage')]));
    return { n: t.rows.length, bi: t.rows.filter((r, i) => bins[i].BI === r[k('BI')]).length, ii: t.rows.filter((r, i) => bins[i].ii === r[k('II')]).length,
      depth: Math.max(...t.rows.map((r, i) => Math.abs(bins[i].depth - r[k('Depth')]))), max: Math.max(...dp), mean: dp.reduce((a, v) => a + v, 0) / dp.length };
  });
  if (v2.bi !== v2.n || v2.ii !== v2.n || v2.depth > 1e-9 || v2.max > 0.6) fail(`web–V2 in the browser: BI ${v2.bi}/${v2.n}, ii ${v2.ii}/${v2.n}, depth ${v2.depth}, max Δ% ${v2.max}`);
  console.log(`web–V2 in the browser: ${v2.n} intervals, BI and ii identical, max Δ ${v2.max.toFixed(2)} points, mean ${v2.mean.toFixed(2)}`);

    let closed = false; window.close = () => { closed = true; }; window.corantoolDesktop = {};
    IDBDatabase.prototype.transaction = function (stores, mode, ...args) {
      if (mode === 'readwrite') throw new DOMException('simulated quota exceeded', 'QuotaExceededError');
      return tx.call(this, stores, mode, ...args);
    };

  // Import a native 16-bit TIFF through the file input and reopen it from the project library.
  await p.evaluate(() => {

    const tags = [[256, 4, w], [257, 4, h], [258, 3, 16], [259, 3, 1], [262, 3, 1],
      [273, 4, start], [277, 3, 1], [278, 4, h], [279, 4, w * h * 2]];
    data.setUint16(0, 0x4949);
    data.setUint16(2, 42, true); data.setUint32(4, 8, true); data.setUint16(8, tags.length, true);
    tags.forEach(([tag, type, value], i) => {
      const offset = 10 + 12 * i;
      data.setUint16(offset, tag, true); data.setUint16(offset + 2, type, true);
      data.setUint32(offset + 4, 1, true); data.setUint32(offset + 8, value, true);
    });
    for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) data.setUint16(start + 2 * i, 32768 + i, true);

  // Force the IndexedDB fallback, verify cross-chunk reads and fail a replacement mid-write.
  const fallback = await p.evaluate(async () => {
    const { putImage, getImage } = await import('./js/storage.js');
    Object.defineProperty(navigator.storage, 'getDirectory', { configurable: true, value: undefined });
    const boundary = 4 * 1024 * 1024, key = 'e2e-image-fallback';
    const source = new Blob([new Uint8Array(boundary - 1), new Uint8Array([14, 27, 42])]);
    try {
      await putImage(key, source);
      let failed = false;
      await putImage(key, {
        size: source.size, type: source.type,
        slice(start, end) {
          if (start > 0) throw new Error('simulated chunk write failure');
          return source.slice(start, end);
        },
      }).catch(() => { failed = true; });
      const stored = await getImage(key, true);
      const sample = new Uint8Array(await stored.slice(boundary - 1, boundary + 2).arrayBuffer());
      const legacy = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const req = indexedDB.open('ichantool');
        req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
        req.onsuccess = () => {
          const db = req.result, tx = db.transaction('images', 'readwrite');
          tx.objectStore('images').put({ key: 'e2e-legacy-image', bytes: new Uint8Array([81]).buffer, type: 'image/tiff' });
          tx.oncomplete = () => { db.close(); resolve(); };
          tx.onabort = () => { db.close(); reject(tx.error); };
        };
      }).then(() => getImage('e2e-legacy-image'));
      return [failed, ...sample, ...new Uint8Array(await legacy.arrayBuffer())];
    } finally { delete navigator.storage.getDirectory; }
  });
  assert.deepEqual(fallback, [true, 14, 27, 42, 81]);

    const encoder = new TextEncoder(), jsonStart = payloadAt(0);
    const nameOffset = encoder.encode(crcProject.slice(0, crcProject.indexOf(crcName))).length;
    const imageStart = payloadAt(jsonStart + encoder.encode(crcProject).length);

    const log = console.error;
    const confirmReplace = window.confirm;
    let rejected = false;
    let replacePrompts = 0;
    const crcErrors = [], crcChecks = [];
    console.error = (error, ...args) => {
      if (String(error).includes('ZIP member name')) rejected = true;
      else if (String(error).includes('ZIP CRC mismatch')) crcErrors.push(String(error));
      else log(error, ...args);
    };
    window.confirm = () => { replacePrompts++; return true; };
    try {
      for (const [name, offset] of [['project.json', jsonStart + nameOffset], [imagePath, imageStart + image.size - 1]]) {
        const changed = bytes.slice(); changed[offset] ^= 1;
        sendFile(new File([changed], 'altered.sedcoretool'));
        const message = 'ZIP CRC mismatch for ' + name;
        const warned = await waitFor(() => crcErrors.some((error) => error.includes(message))
          && document.getElementById('toast').textContent.includes(message));
        crcChecks.push(warned && S.id === crcId && S.name === crcName
          && JSON.stringify(await getProject(crcId)) === beforeCrc && (await listProjects()).length === projectsBeforeCrc);
      }
      await send('../project.json', 'hostile-zip-smoke');
      await waitFor(() => rejected);
      return { opened, saved, rejected, notStored: !(await getProject('hostile-zip-smoke')),
        validQuiet, validBins, currentWarns, legacyWarns, corrected,
        legacyPreview, sourcePreserved, currentPreview, healthyOverlap, undoClear, undoMerge, redoMerge, nativeShortcuts,
        undoClass, invalidatedRedo, healthySave,
        intactZipOpened, crcChecks, replacePrompts };
    } finally { console.error = log; window.confirm = confirmReplace; }
  });
  assert.deepEqual(importZip, { opened: true, saved: true, rejected: true, notStored: true,
    validQuiet: true, validBins: [[0, 1, 0, 0, 1], [1, 2, 0, 0, 1]], currentWarns: true, legacyWarns: true, corrected: true,
    legacyPreview: true, sourcePreserved: true, currentPreview: true, healthyOverlap: true,
    undoClear: true, undoMerge: true, redoMerge: true, nativeShortcuts: true, undoClass: true, invalidatedRedo: true, healthySave: true,
    intactZipOpened: true, crcChecks: [true, true], replacePrompts: 0 });
  if (errs.length) fail('page errors: ' + errs.join(' | '));
  console.log(process.exitCode ? 'E2E finished with failures' : `E2E ok (${first} traces in the first section; reload identical)`);
} catch (e) { fail(e.stack || e.message); } finally { await b.close(); }

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { prepareDataTableFixture, captureDataTableCSV } from '../../tools/data_table_fixture.mjs';

export async function verifyTableViewport(browser, url) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
  const press = async (shortcut) => {
    const keys = shortcut.split('+'), key = keys.pop();
    for (const modifier of keys) await page.keyboard.down(modifier);
    try { await page.keyboard.press(key); }
    finally { for (const modifier of keys.reverse()) await page.keyboard.up(modifier); }
  };
  const frame = () => page.evaluate(() => new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(resolve))));
  const scrollTo = async (row) => {
    await page.evaluate((index) => {
      window.getSelection().removeAllRanges();
      document.querySelector('#dBody .dsgrid').scrollTop = index * 28;
    }, row);
    await frame();
  };
  try {
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(prepareDataTableFixture, 2400);
    await page.click('#modeData');
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#dsViewport', (input) => input.checked), false);
    assert.equal(await page.$$eval('#dBody tbody tr', (rows) => rows.length), 400);
    const bounded = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const { S } = window.ichantool, originals = S.cores.map((core) => core.proxies);
      let reads = 0;
      try {
        for (const core of S.cores) core.proxies = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(core.proxies).map(([name, values]) => [name,
          new Proxy(values, { get(target, property, receiver) { if (/^\d+$/.test(String(property))) reads++; return Reflect.get(target, property, receiver); } }),
        ]));
        document.getElementById('dsViewport').click();
      } finally { S.cores.forEach((core, index) => { core.proxies = originals[index]; }); }
      const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('#dBody tr[data-row]')];
      return { reads, rows: rows.length, heights: rows.map((row) => row.getBoundingClientRect().height) };
    });
    assert.ok(bounded.rows > 0 && bounded.rows < 100);
    assert.equal(bounded.reads, bounded.rows * 6);
    assert.ok(bounded.heights.every((height) => height === 28), JSON.stringify(bounded.heights));
    await page.click('#dsEdit');
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="1"] td[data-col="value_0"]');
    await press('Control+A'); await page.keyboard.type('-901.234567');
    await press('PageDown');
    assert.ok(await page.evaluate(() => Number(document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row) > 1));
    await press('Control+End');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '399');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.dataset.col), 'value_5');
    assert.ok(await page.evaluate(() => {
      const grid = document.querySelector('.dsgrid').getBoundingClientRect();
      const header = document.querySelector('.dsgrid thead th').getBoundingClientRect();
      return Math.abs(header.top - grid.top) <= 2;
    }));
    await press('Shift+Tab');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.dataset.col), 'value_4');
    await press('ArrowUp');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '398');
    await press('Control+Home');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '0');
    await press('PageDown'); await press('PageUp');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '0');
    await page.evaluate(captureDataTableCSV, { rowCount: 2400, edited: { row: 1, column: 0, value: -901.234567 } });
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="1"] td[data-col="value_0"]');
    await page.click('#dsRestoreCell');
    await page.evaluate(captureDataTableCSV, { rowCount: 2400 });
    await page.click('#dMore');
    await page.setViewport({ width: 900, height: 900 });
    await scrollTo(1190);
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="1199"] td[data-col="value_5"]');
    await press('Tab');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '1200');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.dataset.core), 'table-core-1');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.dataset.col), 'value_0');
    await press('Shift+Tab');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '1199');
    assert.ok(await page.evaluate(() => {
      const grid = document.querySelector('.dsgrid').getBoundingClientRect(), cell = document.activeElement.getBoundingClientRect();
      return cell.left >= grid.left && cell.right <= grid.right + 1;
    }));
    await page.setViewport({ width: 1440, height: 900 });
    await scrollTo(1290);
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="1300"] td[data-col="value_1"]');
    await press('Control+A'); await page.keyboard.type('42.125');

    await page.evaluate(captureDataTableCSV, { rowCount: 2400, edited: { row: 1300, column: 1, value: 42.125 } });
    await page.click('#dsRestoreAll'); await page.click('#dsRestoreAll');
    await page.evaluate(captureDataTableCSV, { rowCount: 2400 });
    await scrollTo(0);
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="2"] td[data-col="value_0"]');
    const pasted = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const cell = document.activeElement, range = document.createRange(); range.selectNodeContents(cell);
      window.getSelection().removeAllRanges(); window.getSelection().addRange(range);
      document.execCommand('insertHTML', false, '<div>1</div><div>2</div>');
      return { text: cell.textContent, height: cell.closest('tr').getBoundingClientRect().height };
    });
    assert.deepEqual(pasted, { text: '12', height: 28 });
    await press('Escape');
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="2"] td[data-col="value_0"]');

      const range = document.createRange(); range.selectNodeContents(cell);

      return selection.toString();
    });
    await page.evaluate(() => { document.querySelector('#dBody .dsgrid').scrollTop = 1350 * 28; });
    await frame();
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.getSelection().toString()), selected);
    assert.ok(await page.$$eval('tr[data-row]', (rows) => rows.length > 1000));
    await scrollTo(1399);

    await scrollTo(0);
    const point = await page.evaluate(() => {

      const grid = document.querySelector('#dBody .dsgrid').getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: cell.left + 12, y: cell.top + 12, bottom: grid.bottom - 8 };
    });

    for (let step = 0; step < 4; step++) {
      await page.mouse.wheel({ deltaY: 420 }); await frame();
      await page.mouse.move(point.x + step % 2, point.bottom);
    }
    await page.mouse.up(); await frame();
    const drag = await page.evaluate(() => {
      const selection = window.getSelection();
      const row = (node) => (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE ? node : node.parentElement).closest('tr[data-row]');
      const anchor = row(selection.anchorNode), focus = row(selection.focusNode);
      const from = Number(anchor?.dataset.row), to = Number(focus?.dataset.row);
      const contiguous = Number.isFinite(from) && Number.isFinite(to) && Array.from({ length: Math.abs(to - from) + 1 }, (_, i) =>
        document.querySelector(`tr[data-row="${Math.min(from, to) + i}"]`)).every(Boolean);
      return { from, to, text: selection.toString().length, contiguous };
    });
    assert.ok(drag.text > 0 && drag.to > drag.from + 40 && drag.contiguous, JSON.stringify(drag));
    await scrollTo(1399);

    assert.equal(await page.$eval('tr[data-row="99999"] td[data-col]', (cell) => cell.dataset.x), '49999');
    assert.ok(await page.$$eval('tr[data-row]', (rows) => rows.length < 100));
    await page.focus('tr[data-row="99999"] td[data-col="value_0"]');
    await press('Control+Home');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '0');
    await press('Control+End');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.closest('tr').dataset.row), '99999');
    assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    console.log('Viewport table: bounded rows, native selection/drag, scrolling edits, keyboard navigation, filters, rollback and age-only cores verified.');
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/e2e/taxon-figures.mjs

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { objectStats, DEFAULT_FILTERS, DEFAULT_DETECT } from '../../app/js/imageproc.js';
import { rleEncode } from '../../app/js/core.js';

export function taxonFigureFixture() {
  const width = 8, height = 100, labels = new Int32Array(width * height), rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(width * height * 4).fill(180);
  for (let y = 10; y < 30; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 4; x++) labels[y * width + x] = 1;
  for (let y = 40; y < 60; y++) for (let x = 4; x < 8; x++) labels[y * width + x] = 2;
  const stats = objectStats(labels, width, height, rgba);
  return { app: 'SedCoreTool', kind: 'project', version: 5, id: 'taxon-figures', name: 'Synthetic ichnotaxa',
    classes: [{ id: 'ch', name: 'Chondrites', color: '#126682', counts: true, symbol: 'circle' },
      { id: 'pl', name: 'Planolites', color: '#d46b28', counts: true, symbol: 'diamond' },
      { id: 'zo', name: 'Zoophycos', color: '#386a39', counts: true, symbol: 'tri' }],
    settings: { binCm: 1, minValid: 20, tips: false, idMode: false },
    cores: [{ id: 'taxon-core', name: 'Synthetic core', units: 'cm', age: { text: '0, 0\n10, 20', units: 'ka' },
      logView: { height: 500, tracks: { image: false, total: true, species: true, BI: false, ii: false, manual: false, marks: false } },
      sections: [{ id: 'taxon-section', name: 'Synthetic section', w: width, h: height, ds: 1, top: 0, bottom: 10, units: 'cm',
        roi: { x0: 0, x1: width, y0: 0, y1: height }, filters: DEFAULT_FILTERS, detect: DEFAULT_DETECT, nextId: 3,
        labels: rleEncode(labels), exclude: [0, width * height],
        objects: [1, 2].map((id) => ({ id, cls: id === 1 ? 'ch' : 'pl', src: 'draw', st: stats.get(id) })),
        marks: [{ id: 1, x: 4, y: 75, cls: 'ch' }, { id: 2, x: 4, y: 85, cls: 'zo' }],
      }] }] };
}

/** Runs against the served app or the standalone file; browser-side helpers use no module imports. */
export async function verifyTaxonFigures(browser, url, { screenshots = false } = {}) {
  const page = await browser.newPage(), errors = [];
  page.setDefaultTimeout(15000);
  const history = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (error) => errors.push(error.message));
  const setOption = async (scope, key, value) => {
    const selector = `[data-taxa-scope="${scope}"] [data-taxa="${key}"]`;
    history.push(await page.evaluate((selector, next) => {
      const element = document.querySelector(selector);
      if (element.type === 'checkbox') element.checked = next;
      else element.value = String(next);
      const input = { field: element.dataset.taxa, type: element.type, value: element.value, connected: element.isConnected, panel: !!element.closest('#figPanel') };
      element.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
      return { ...input, settings: window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles, column: window.ichantool.S.logView.style?.taxa };
    }, selector, value));
  };
  const snapshot = () => page.evaluate(() => {
    const I = window.ichantool;
    return JSON.stringify(I.S.sections.map((section) => {
      I.analyze(section);
      return { labels: [...section.labels], exclude: [...section.exclude], marks: section.marks,
        assignments: [...section.objects.values()].map((object) => [object.id, object.cls]), bins: section.bins };
    }));
  });
  try {
    await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.ichantool);
    await page.evaluate(async (fixture) => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width = 8; canvas.height = 100;
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d'); context.fillStyle = '#b4b4b4'; context.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 100);
      const blob = await new Promise((resolve) => canvas.toBlob(resolve));
      fixture.cores[0].sections[0].file = { name: 'synthetic.png', type: 'image/png', blob };
      const I = window.ichantool; await I.loadProject(fixture);
      document.getElementById('home').hidden = true;
      I.S.fig.type = 'core'; I.S.fig.coreH = 500; I.setMode('fig');
    }, taxonFigureFixture());
    await page.waitForSelector('#figOut svg');
    const original = await snapshot();
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles == null), true);
    const coreTab = await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTab);
    await setOption('figure', 'abundance', 'kite');
    await setOption('figure', 'opacity', 40);
    await setOption('figure', 'occurrence', 'names');
    await setOption('figure', 'source', 'marks');
    await setOption('figure', 'scale', 'percent');
    await setOption('figure', 'values', true);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"] polygon')
      && document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="percentage"]')
      && document.querySelectorAll('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"]').length === 2);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#figOut [data-role="abundance"]', (group) => group.getAttribute('opacity')), '0.4');
    assert.equal(await page.$$eval('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"]', (groups) => groups.every((group) => group.dataset.kind === 'mark' && !group.querySelector('path'))), true);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#figOut [data-taxon="Zoophycos"][data-role="abundance"]', (group) => group.children.length), 0);
    if (screenshots) await page.screenshot({ path: 'dist/taxon-core.png' });
    await page.click('#figTabs [data-ftabadd]');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTabs.length), 2);
    await page.select('#figType', 'range');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles?.range == null), true);
    await setOption('figure', 'abundance', 'bars');
    await setOption('figure', 'occurrence', 'symbols');
    await setOption('figure', 'source', 'all');
    await setOption('figure', 'scale', 'percent');
    await setOption('figure', 'values', true);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"] rect')
      && document.querySelectorAll('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"]').length === 4);
    assert.equal(await page.$eval('#figOut [data-role="abundance"]', (group) => group.getAttribute('opacity')), '1');
    assert.equal(await page.$$eval('#figOut [data-role="percentage"]', (labels) => labels.some((label) => label.textContent === '50%')), true);
    await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('[data-taxa-scope="figure"] [data-taxa-all="0"]').click());
    await page.waitForFunction(() => !document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"]'));
    await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('[data-taxa-scope="figure"] [data-taxa-all="1"]').click());
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"]').length === 4);
    if (screenshots) await page.screenshot({ path: 'dist/taxon-distribution.png' });
    const rangeTab = await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.figTab);
    await page.click(`[data-ftab="${coreTab}"] .ftl`);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"][opacity="0.4"] polygon'));
    await page.click('#modeLog'); await page.select('#logAxis', 'age');
    await setOption('column', 'abundance', 'none'); await setOption('column', 'occurrence', 'names'); await setOption('column', 'source', 'marks');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#logSvg [data-role="occurrence"] title')?.textContent === 'Chondrites: 15');
    await page.click('#modeFig');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"] title')?.textContent === 'Chondrites: 15');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles.core.opacity), 0.4);
    await page.evaluate(() => {
      window.__taxonExports = [];
      const createURL = URL.createObjectURL;
      URL.createObjectURL = (blob) => { if (['image/svg+xml', 'image/png'].includes(blob.type)) window.__taxonExports.push(blob); return createURL(blob); };
      document.addEventListener('click', (event) => { if (event.target.closest?.('a[download]')) event.preventDefault(); }, true);
    });
    await page.click('#figSvg'); await page.click('#figPng');
    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__taxonExports.some((blob) => blob.type === 'image/png'));
    const exported = await page.evaluate(async () => {
      const svg = await window.__taxonExports.find((blob) => blob.type === 'image/svg+xml').text();
      const png = window.__taxonExports.find((blob) => blob.type === 'image/png'), image = await createImageBitmap(png);
      return { svg, bytes: [...new Uint8Array(await png.arrayBuffer()).slice(0, 8)], width: image.width, height: image.height };
    });
    assert.match(exported.svg, /data-role="abundance" opacity="0.4"/);
    assert.deepEqual(exported.bytes, [137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]); assert.ok(exported.width > 0 && exported.height > 0);
    assert.equal(await snapshot(), original);
    const saved = await page.evaluate(async () => { await window.ichantool.saveToDB(); return window.ichantool.serialize(); });
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    await page.evaluate(async (project) => {
      await window.ichantool.loadProject(project); document.getElementById('home').hidden = true; window.ichantool.setMode('fig');
    }, saved);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"][opacity="0.4"] polygon'));
    assert.equal(await snapshot(), original);
    await page.evaluate((id) => document.querySelector(`[data-ftab="${id}"] .ftl`).click(), rangeTab);
    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('#figOut [data-role="abundance"][opacity="1"] rect'));
    await page.click('[data-taxa-scope="figure"] [data-taxa-reset]');
    assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => window.ichantool.S.fig.taxaStyles.range == null), true);
    assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
    console.log('Ichnotaxon figures: shapes, manual names, percentages, independent tabs, depth/age, exact analysis, save/reload and SVG/PNG verified.');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Ichnotaxon page errors:', errors);
    console.error('Ichnotaxon control history:', JSON.stringify(history));
    console.error('Ichnotaxon figure state:', JSON.stringify(await page.evaluate(() => ({
      active: window.ichantool.S.figTab, tabs: window.ichantool.S.figTabs.map((tab) => ({ id: tab.id, type: tab.fig.type, taxa: tab.fig.taxaStyles })),
      output: document.getElementById('figOut').innerHTML.slice(0, 500),
      occurrences: document.querySelectorAll('#figOut [data-role="occurrence"]').length,
      polygons: document.querySelectorAll('#figOut [data-role="abundance"] polygon').length,
      percentages: document.querySelectorAll('#figOut [data-role="percentage"]').length,
    }))));
    throw error;
  } finally { await page.close(); }
}


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/fixtures/make_unet_parity.py
##########################################

"""Writes unet_parity.json: reference outputs of the shared U-Net tiling rules (V0.15).
Run from the repository root: python tests/fixtures/make_unet_parity.py. Python and JS tests compare against it."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from sedcoretool.unet.tiling import UnetConfig, blend_window, predict_proba, resample_bilinear, tile_starts

W, H, ROI = 50, 37, (3, 2, 47, 35)  # x0, y0, x1, y1


def image():
    y, x = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    return np.stack([(x * 37 + y * 11) % 256, (x * 5 + y * 29) % 256, (x * y) % 256], -1).astype(np.uint8)


def run1(t):
    return (t[0] - 0.5 * t[2])[None]


def run3(t):
    return np.stack([t[0], t[1], t[2]])


def compute():
    img = image()
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = ROI
    crop = img[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    cfg = UnetConfig(tile=16, overlap=4)
    p1, _ = predict_proba(run1, crop, cfg, 1.3)
    p3, c3 = predict_proba(run3, crop, cfg, 1.0)
    return {"w": W, "h": H, "roi": ROI, "tile": 16, "overlap": 4,
            "tileStarts": [tile_starts(n, t, o) for n, t, o in [(10, 16, 4), (16, 16, 4), (17, 16, 4), (100, 32, 8), (95, 32, 8)]],
            "blend": [round(float(v), 6) for v in blend_window(8, 3)],
            "resampled": resample_bilinear(img, 23, 31).reshape(-1).tolist(),
            "prob1": [round(float(v), 5) for v in p1.reshape(-1)],
            "prob3": [round(float(v), 5) for v in p3.reshape(-1)], "cls3": c3.reshape(-1).tolist()}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    out = Path(__file__).with_name("unet_parity.json")
    out.write_text(json.dumps(compute(), separators=(",", ":")), encoding="utf-8")
    print(out)

"""Scientific equivalence and rejection boundaries of the analysis profiling harness."""

import copy
import importlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def analysis_benchmark():
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as patch:
        patch.syspath_prepend(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tools"))
        return importlib.import_module("benchmark_analysis")


@pytest.fixture
def snapshot(analysis_benchmark):
    from benchmark import rle

    labels = np.full((6, 6), 2, dtype=np.int32)
    labels[1:5, 1:5] = [[1, 0, 3, 2], [1, 2, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0, 0], [2, 2, 2, 2]]
    exclude = np.zeros((6, 6), dtype=np.uint8)
    exclude[3:5, 1:5] = 1
    return {"classes": ["a", "b"], "settings": {"binCm": 2, "minValid": 0.2, "noDataPad": 0},
            "sections": [{"name": "cropped", "w": 6, "h": 6, "top": 0, "bottom": 4, "units": "cm",
                          "roi": {"x0": 1, "x1": 5, "y0": 1, "y1": 5},
                          "eff": [-1, 0, 1, -1], "labels": rle(labels), "exclude": rle(exclude)}]}


def test_worker_profiles_the_public_api_with_cropped_classes_exclusions_and_partial_bins(analysis_benchmark, snapshot, tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "snapshot.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(snapshot), encoding="utf-8")
    before = path.read_bytes()
    result = analysis_benchmark.python_worker(path, profiled=True)
    assert result["inputs_unchanged"] and path.read_bytes() == before
    normal, fractional = result["scenarios"]
    assert normal["bins"] == [[[0, 2, 1, 100, 50, 25, 25, 3, 4], [2, 4, 3, 0, None, None, None, None, None]]]
    assert fractional["bins"] == [[[0, 1, 0.5, 100, 50, 25, 25, 3, 4],
                                    [1, 3, 2, 50, 50, 25, 25, 3, 4],
                                    [3, 4, 3.5, 0, None, None, None, None, None]]]
    for scenario in result["scenarios"]:
        calls = [site for site in scenario["cpu"]["sites"] if site["function"] == "analyze_mask"]
        assert len(calls) == 1 and calls[0]["calls"] == scenario["cpu"]["passes"] == 5


@pytest.mark.parametrize("labels", [[-1, 36], [4, 36]])
def test_unmapped_or_overlapping_fixture_labels_fail_before_remapping(analysis_benchmark, snapshot, labels):
    snapshot["sections"][0]["labels"] = labels
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mapped object labels"):
        analysis_benchmark.prepare_inputs(snapshot)


def test_result_validation_rejects_wrong_indices_and_changed_repetitions(analysis_benchmark):
    bins = [[[0, 2, 1, 100, 50, 25, 25, 3, 3]]]
    result = {"scenarios": [{"bin_cm": size, "bins": bins} for size in analysis_benchmark.BIN_CM], "inputs_unchanged": True}
    reference, _ = analysis_benchmark.validate_results(result, copy.deepcopy(result))
    wrong_index = copy.deepcopy(result)
    wrong_index["scenarios"][0]["bins"][0][0][-1] = 4
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="BI/ii differ"):
        analysis_benchmark.validate_results(result, wrong_index)
    different_results = copy.deepcopy(result)
    different_results["scenarios"][0]["bins"][0][0][4] += 1
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="between trials"):
        analysis_benchmark.validate_results(different_results, copy.deepcopy(different_results), reference)
    roundoff = copy.deepcopy(result)
    roundoff["scenarios"][0]["bins"][0][0][4] += 1e-10
    analysis_benchmark.validate_results(result, roundoff)
    roundoff["scenarios"][0]["bins"][0][0][4] += 1e-6
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Numerical mismatch"):
        analysis_benchmark.validate_results(result, roundoff)


def test_fractional_diagnostics_preserve_failed_parity_and_full_evidence(analysis_benchmark):
    bins = [[[0, 1, 0.5, 100, 50, 25, 25, 3, 4]]]
    browser = {"scenarios": [{"bin_cm": size, "bins": copy.deepcopy(bins)} for size in analysis_benchmark.BIN_CM],
               "inputs_unchanged": True}
    python = copy.deepcopy(browser)
    python["scenarios"][1]["bins"][0][0][1] = 2
    _, parity = analysis_benchmark.validate_results(browser, python, diagnose_fractional=True)
    assert parity[0]["passed"] is True and parity[1]["passed"] is False
    assert parity[1]["differences"] == [{"section": 0, "interval": 0, "column": 1, "javascript": 1, "python": 2}]
    assert parity[1]["javascript_bins"] == bins
    assert parity[1]["python_bins"] == python["scenarios"][1]["bins"]
    python["scenarios"][0]["bins"][0][0][1] = 2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Numerical mismatch"):
        analysis_benchmark.validate_results(browser, python, diagnose_fractional=True)


##########################################
ARCHIVO: tests/test_batch.py
